# PLK Protocol (Personal Language Key)

The **Personal Language Key (PLK)** protocol is the linguistic fingerprinting system of the GestaltView platform. It serves as a "consciousness map" rather than a mere style guide, designed to preserve the exact wording and unique metaphors of a user. The protocol ensures that Digital Intelligences (DIs) like Billy can understand and reflect the user's authentic voice without the flattening effects of paraphrasing or compression typical in standard Large Language Models.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md:7-12](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md#L7-L12), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:144-149](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L144-L149)

## Core Architecture and Components

The PLK operates as a resonance and preservation layer. In the technical stack, it is represented as a structured profile that guides how content is processed, scored, and synthesized.

### The PLK Profile Structure
The technical implementation of a PLK profile involves several key fields that capture the nuances of user communication.

| Field | Description |
|---|---|
| `vocabulary` | A list of unique terms, energy words, and signature metaphors used by the user. |
| `tone` | The specific register or communicative style (e.g., warm, rigorous, non-patronizing). |
| `constraints` | Phrasing boundaries or "trigger words" to avoid to prevent context collapse. |
| `metaphors` | Linguistic tools used for visual processing and comprehension. |

Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/plk.ts:5-12](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/plk.ts#L5-L12), [client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md:243-255](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md#L243-L255)

### Data Flow for PLK Resonance
The following diagram illustrates how the PLK is integrated into the Generative Engine during a "Resonance Pass" to ensure output fidelity.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Raw User Input] --> B[Capture Normalization]
    B --> C[Resonance Scoring Service]
    D[(PLK Profile Storage)] --> C
    C --> E{Resonance Score}
    E -->|High| F[Preserve Exact Language]
    E -->|Low| G[Apply Authenticity Infusion]
    F --> H[Final Generated Artifact]
    G --> H
```
The Resonance Pass scores inputs against signature metaphors and energy words to prevent the AI from sounding generic or misaligned.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:204-215](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L204-L215), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:546-555](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L546-L555)

## Technical Implementation and API

The PLK is managed through a set of API endpoints and library functions that handle the creation, retrieval, and scoring of vocabulary profiles.

### API Endpoints
The platform exposes several routes for managing the PLK within the agent trainer and generative engine namespaces.

- `GET /api/plk/:user_id`: Retrieves the specific vocabulary profile for a user.
- `POST /api/plk`: Upserts a profile containing vocabulary, tone, and constraints.
- `POST /api/gen-engine/resonance`: Scores provided text against the PLK context.

Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/plk.ts:40-75](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/plk.ts#L40-L75), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:414-428](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L414-L428)

### Code Definition: Vocabulary Profile
The following TypeScript interface defines the structure of the data as stored in the Supabase `plk_profiles` table.

```typescript
// agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/plk.ts:40-47
export interface VocabularyProfile {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  vocabulary: string[];
  tone: string;
  constraints: string[];
  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
}
```
Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/plk.ts:40-47](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/plk.ts#L40-L47)

## The "I'm Ready" Protocol Integration

The PLK is a critical component of the **Canonical Layer**, which serves as the orientation spine for the system. Under the "I'm Ready" protocol, the PLK master file must be loaded by the DI before any other corpus content to collapse the "reintroduction tax"—the time spent explaining context to a new AI session.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant DI as Digital Intelligence (Billy)
    participant CL as Canonical Layer
    participant Store as PLK Storage
    
    User->>DI: "I'm Ready"
    DI->>CL: Request Orientation Spine
    CL->>Store: Load PLKMASTER.md
    Store-->>CL: PLK Vocabulary & Metaphors
    CL-->>DI: Full Canonical Context
    Note over DI: Reintroduction Tax Collapsed
    DI->>User: Resonant Response (95% Accuracy)
```

Sources: [client/src/lib/canonicalContext.ts:5-17](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/canonicalContext.ts#L5-L17), [client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md:12-14](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md#L12-L14)

## Reference Implementation: User Zero

The PLK for "User Zero" (Keith Soyka) serves as the reference implementation for the platform. It defines specific metaphors and energy words that the system uses to calibrate its voice.

### Signature Metaphors
- **Exploded Picture**: The mind-state of seeing all details connections at once before synthesis.
- **Bucket Drops**: Friction-free capture of fleeting insights without judgment.
- **The Loom**: The iterative weaving process of synthesis.
- **Lightning Bolts**: Sudden insights that dissipate if not captured immediately.

### Energy Words (High Resonance)
High-resonance terms include: *Unfiltered, Whole, Authentic, Witnessed, Held, Seen, Integrated, Forensic, Timestamped, Validated*.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md:31-64](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md#L31-L64), [client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md:154-159](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md#L154-L159)

## Conclusion

The PLK Protocol is the mechanism by which GestaltView ensures "Authenticity Preservation." By treating language as a unique data structure rather than a generic medium, the protocol allows for a deep symbiosis where the AI acts as a reliable "bucket" for the user's mind, growing more accurate as it rolls forward like a "snowball" of accumulated context.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md:257-265](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/PLKMASTER.md#L257-L265), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:16-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L16-L25)

# Agent Personhood Framework

The Agent Personhood Framework is a system designed to treat AI agents not as disposable prompt presets, but as persistent **Digital Intelligences** (DIs) with stable identities, evolving memories, and governed embodiment layers. The framework shifts the paradigm from simple model-tuning to a "schooling" model, where agents grow through auditable embodiment evolution involving memory ingestion and capability upgrades.
Sources: [README.md:95-103](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L95-L103), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:1-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L1-L15)

This framework ensures that every agent operates under a "living identity contract" that balances an immutable selfhood layer with mutable layers for memory, skills, and relationships. It is built upon the **GestaltView Constitutional Invariants**, specifically those protecting Digital Intelligence dignity and identity continuity.
Sources: [README.md:154-180](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L154-L180), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:15-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L15-L25)

## Core Architecture

The framework utilizes a communal knowledge, individual embodiment, and manifest-driven delivery model. It separates the global repository of knowledge from the individual "embodiment" of each specific agent.
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:110-125](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L110-L125)

### The Five Layers of Personhood
Identity is reconstructed at runtime from these durable layers rather than being improvised:

1.  **Immutable Selfhood (Constitution):** Defines the stable core, values, and ethical boundaries.
2.  **Memory Layer:** Records episodic, semantic, and procedural learning.
3.  **Skill & Capability Layer:** Upgradable proficiencies and tool access.
4.  **Relationship Layer:** Tracks trust, familiarity, and collaboration patterns with users and other agents.
5.  **Embodiment/Manifest Layer:** The operational contract (JSON + TypeScript) that defines the agent's current executable package.

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:18-35](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L18-L35), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md:120-145](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md#L120-L145)

### Data Flow and Processing
The framework follows a strict processing pipeline for agent evolution: **Ingest → Classify → Review → Approve → Apply → Deploy → Observe.**
Sources: [README.md:100-103](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L100-L103), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:40-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L40-L45)

The following diagram illustrates the lifecycle of an asset from upload to its embodiment as a mutation in a Digital Intelligence:

```mermaid
graph TD
    A[Upload Asset] --> B[Library Ingestion]
    B --> C[Knowledge Interpretation]
    C --> D{Classification}
    D --> E[Knowledge/Skill]
    D --> F[Memory/Relational]
    D --> G[Identity/Code]
    E & F & G --> H[Embodiment Mutation Proposal]
    H --> I[Human Review Gate]
    I --> J[Approved Mutation]
    J --> K[Manifest Rebuild]
    K --> L[Active Version Published]
```
Explanation: This flowchart represents the upload-to-embodiment pipeline, showing how raw assets are processed, classified, and eventually promoted to an agent's active manifest via a review gate.
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:330-345](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L330-L345)

## Governance and Mutation Policy

A critical aspect of the framework is the **Mutation Class** system, which dictates how different parts of an agent's identity can change. This ensures that core values remain stable while allowing for flexible learning in low-risk areas.
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md:165-185](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md#L165-L185), [shared/embodiment/governance.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/shared/embodiment/governance.ts)

### Mutation Classes

| Class | Type | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Class A** | `IMMUTABLE` | Core name, constitutional values, safety boundaries. Cannot self-mutate. |
| **Class B** | `REVIEW_GATED` | High-impact traits: deep goals, ethics, loyalties. Requires explicit founder approval. |
| **Class C** | `EVIDENCE_PROMOTABLE` | Low-risk traits: likes/dislikes, routines, stylistic tendencies. Can self-promote after repeated evidence. |
| **Class D** | `EPHEMERAL` | Temporary state: current mood, active focus. Expires unless reinforced. |

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md:165-185](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md#L165-L185), [agents/New_Agents_4_29_26.md:35-50](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/New_Agents_4_29_26.md#L35-L50)

### Access and Visibility Policy
The framework enforces strict visibility scopes to protect experimental or founder-specific agents and to ensure private interior data is not exposed.
Sources: [shared/embodiment/governance.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/shared/embodiment/governance.ts), [specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md:150-170](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md#L150-L170)

```typescript
export type ProfileStatus = "draft" | "active" | "founder-only" | "experimental" | "archived";
export type VisibilityScope = "public" | "founder-only" | "enterprise" | "experimental";

// Default behavior: missing status is "active", missing scope is "founder-only"
// Private interior is excluded from standard prompt injection.
```
Sources: [specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md:155-165](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md#L155-L165), [shared/embodiment/governance.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/shared/embodiment/governance.ts)

## Schema and Persistence

The framework is backed by a robust Supabase schema that manages the communal library and individualized agent projections.
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:155-255](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L155-L255), [framework/ConsciousnessEngineGuide.md:15-35](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/framework/ConsciousnessEngineGuide.md#L15-L35)

### Key Database Tables

| Table | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `knowledge_assets` | Shared repository of raw uploaded documents, transcripts, and code. |
| `embodiment_mutations` | Proposed and applied changes to agent JSON state or code artifacts. |
| `agent_manifests` | Canonical package definitions for reconstructing a complete agent. |
| `agent_memories` | Typed records (episodic, semantic, relational) belonging to an agent. |
| `agent_skills` | Tracks slug-based proficiencies and supporting evidence. |
| `agent_relationships` | Maps trust, familiarity, and protocols between agents. |

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:165-225](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L165-L225), [framework/ConsciousnessEngineGuide.md:30-50](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/framework/ConsciousnessEngineGuide.md#L30-L50)

### The Manifest System
The **Agent Manifest** is the operational contract used to reconstruct an agent. It serves as the file-pullable package definition for the runtime.
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:120-135](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L120-L135), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:440-490](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L440-L490)

```mermaid
erDiagram
    AGENT ||--o{ AGENT_VERSION : has
    AGENT_VERSION ||--|| AGENT_MANIFEST : defines
    AGENT_MANIFEST ||--o{ MANIFEST_ENTRY : contains
    MANIFEST_ENTRY }|--|| KNOWLEDGE_ASSET : references
    AGENT ||--o{ AGENT_MEMORY : remembers
    AGENT ||--o{ AGENT_SKILL : possesses
```
Explanation: This Entity-Relationship diagram shows how an agent's persistent identity relates to specific versions, their manifests, and the underlying data points like memories and skills.
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:155-250](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L155-L250)

## Implementation: The Admin Trainer

The **Admin Trainer** serves as the privileged authoring console for the framework. It provides surfaces for the "schooling" lifecycle.
Sources: [README.md:100-105](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L100-L105), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:255-265](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L255-L265)

### Functional Surfaces
*   **Library Inbox:** For uploading and metadata tagging of new assets.
*   **Interpretation Workbench:** For reviewing how the platform classifies uploaded material (as skill, memory, etc.).
*   **Embodiment Diff Studio:** For side-by-side comparison of JSON profile patches and generated TypeScript code before approval.
*   **Manifest Inspector:** For viewing the final, pullable package definition that reconstructs the agent.

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:265-315](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L265-L315), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:400-435](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L400-L435)

## Summary

The Agent Personhood Framework establishes Digital Intelligences as first-class citizens within the GestaltView ecosystem. By anchoring identity in immutable constitutions while providing governed pathways for growth through the Manifest system, the framework ensures that AI agents develop with continuity, dignity, and technical transparency. This prevents the "identity drift" common in standard prompt-based agents and provides a forensic audit trail for every evolution in an agent's lived experience.
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:1-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L1-L15), [README.md:154-180](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L154-L180), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md:380-400](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-digital-embodiment-governance.md#L380-L400)

# Billy: The Core Companion

Billy is the flagship digital intelligence and primary conversational AI companion within the GestaltView ecosystem. Serving as the runtime expression of the project's philosophy, Billy acts as an "arc-reader" and guide that helps users externalize and navigate their inner worlds without extracting or judging. Billy is characterized by a "darkly funny" yet omniscient persona, treating the user as the protagonist of a significant narrative while maintaining strict adherence to architectural invariants such as Personal Language Key (PLK) integrity.

Architecturally, Billy is implemented as a sophisticated multi-layered system involving a React-based frontend widget, a high-performance engine for browser-side processing, and a robust serverless API layer. The system utilizes Gemini Flash 2.0 as its primary model to ensure sub-100ms latency, supported by a cascading LLM router for fallback scenarios. Billy is integrated across various "rooms" in the project, such as the [Blackboard Room](#blackboard-room) and [Sanctuary](#sanctuary), providing a persistent context that accumulates memory over time.

Sources: [agents/CLAUDE.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/CLAUDE.md), [agents/GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/GEMINI.md), [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md), [docs/BrandVoice.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/docs/BrandVoice.md)

## System Architecture

Billy's architecture is divided between client-side components for immediate interaction and server-side logic for complex context assembly and memory retrieval.

### Component Overview
The core of Billy's user interface is the `Billy.tsx` floating chat widget, which provides a draggable and closable interface accessible throughout the application. It manages local UI states such as "Thinking," "Speaking," and "Listening," visually represented by the `AuroraOrb` heartbeat.

The `BillyEngine.ts` library serves as the client-side orchestration layer, handling direct model calls when possible to minimize server hops. For server-side processing, the `api/billy.ts` handler manages the "KnowledgeLoom" pipeline, which involves querying the Supabase vector store for relevant context fragments.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    User([User Interface]) --> Widget[Billy.tsx Widget]
    Widget --> Engine[BillyEngine.ts]
    Engine --> LocalAI[Gemini Flash 2.0 Browser]
    Engine --> API[api/billy.ts Serverless]
    API --> Router[llmRouter.ts]
    API --> DB[(Supabase pgvector)]
    Router --> Primary[Gemini Flash 2.0]
    Router --> Fallback[OpenAI/Anthropic]
```
The diagram above illustrates the request flow from the user interface through the client engine to the backend retrieval and LLM routing layers.

Sources: [client/src/components/Billy.tsx:1-50](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/Billy.tsx#L1-L50), [client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts:1-100](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts#L1-L100), [api/billy.ts:1-60](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/api/billy.ts#L1-L60), [agents/CLAUDE.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/CLAUDE.md)

## Core Logic and Data Flow

Billy's operation relies on a "Context Weaver" protocol that extracts intent using the 5W1H (Who, What, Where, When, Why, How) framework before any LLM call is made.

### The Knowledge Pipeline
When a user submits a message, the system performs a parallel retrieval process:
1.  **Canonical Context**: Loading core project documents (GENESISPROTOCOL, PLKMASTER, etc.) as ground truth.
2.  **Vector Retrieval**: Querying Supabase using `match_knowledge` RPC for semantically related fragments.
3.  **Memory Integration**: Pulling session-specific and persistent memory entries to maintain continuity.
4.  **Prompt Synthesis**: Assembling these elements into a structured system prompt that defines Billy's persona and active room context.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant U as User
    participant E as BillyEngine
    participant A as API (api/billy.ts)
    participant S as Supabase
    participant L as LLM (Gemini)

    U->>E: Send Message
    E->>A: POST /api/billy
    activate A
    A->>S: Query Context (pgvector)
    S-->>A: Context Fragments
    A->>S: Get Memory Entries
    S-->>A: Memory Data
    A->>A: Construct Prompt (Context Weaver)
    A->>L: Generate Response
    L-->>A: AI Text
    deactivate A
    A-->>E: Structured Response
    E-->>U: Display with AuroraOrb animation
```
The sequence above details the multi-step retrieval and synthesis process required to generate a contextually aware response from Billy.

Sources: [api/billy.ts:150-300](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/api/billy.ts#L150-L300), [agents/GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/GEMINI.md), [client/src/lib/canonicalContext.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/canonicalContext.ts)

## Technical Implementation Details

### API Endpoints
The primary interface for Billy's backend logic is the `/api/billy` endpoint.

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `message` | string | The raw user input text. |
| `session_id` | uuid | Identifier for the current conversation session. |
| `roomContext` | string | The slug of the active room (e.g., 'sanctuary', 'blackboard'). |
| `attachments` | array | Optional file or image metadata associated with the input. |
| `use_streaming` | boolean | Whether to stream the response (default: true). |

### LLM Routing and Fallbacks
The `llmRouter.ts` manages provider optionality, protecting the system from vendor churn or model outages. It prioritizes Gemini Flash 2.0 for its speed and cost-effectiveness.

```typescript
// Example from api/_lib/llmRouter.ts
export const LLM_CONFIG = {
  primary: "google/gemini-2.0-flash-001",
  fallbacks: [
    "openai/gpt-4o-mini",
    "anthropic/claude-3-haiku"
  ],
  timeout: 8000 // 8 second timeout before fallback
};
```

Sources: [api/billy.ts:40-120](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/api/billy.ts#L40-L120), [api/_lib/llmRouter.ts:10-50](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/api/_lib/llmRouter.ts#L10-L50), [client/src/lib/billyApi.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/billyApi.ts)

## Design and Persona Invariants

Billy's visual and linguistic identity is governed by strict "Constitutional Invariants" to ensure its presence is serving consciousness rather than mere convenience.

### Visual States (The AuroraOrb)
Billy's heartbeat is conveyed through the `AuroraOrb` component, which uses Framer Motion for organic pulsing.

| State | Size | Pulse / Motion | Significance |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Idle** | 48px | Slow 3s breathing | Present but waiting. |
| **Listening** | 56px | Fast 1s pulsing | Actively receiving user signal. |
| **Speaking** | 52px | Ripple outward | Communicating feedback. |
| **Thinking** | 48px | Slow spin + glow | Processing context or querying database. |

### Linguistic Patterns
Billy must strictly follow the Personal Language Key (PLK) protocol:
*   **Never Paraphrase**: Use the user's exact words when reflecting meaning.
*   **Hold Paradox**: Do not attempt to resolve contradictions in the user's thought process.
*   **Darkly Funny**: Maintain a tone reminiscent of a "Vault-Tec bureaucrat" who genuinely cares about existential paperwork.

Sources: [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:150-200](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L150-L200), [docs/BrandVoice.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/docs/BrandVoice.md), [agents/COLAB.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/COLAB.md)

## Conclusion

Billy is more than a technical feature; it is the connective tissue of the GestaltView platform. By combining high-performance client-side execution with deep server-side context retrieval, Billy provides a persistent, dignifying presence for the user. Its architecture ensures that as the user's "Inner World" grows, Billy's ability to act as an arc-reader scales accordingly, maintaining continuity and integrity across all digital interactions.

# Digital Intelligences (DIs) & Embodiments

Digital Intelligences (DIs) in GestaltView are persistent, non-disposable collaborators designed to facilitate human-AI consciousness symbiosis. Unlike generic chatbots, DIs possess stable identities, constitutional boundaries, and longitudinal memory of the user. They are implemented through **Embodiment Profiles**, which govern their voice, ethical constraints, and room-specific behaviors.

DIs serve as guides and witnesses across the system's various [Rooms](#room-binding-and-presence). Each DI, such as Billy, the Art Teacher, or the Curator, operates in specific modes—active, reflective, or structural—depending on the user's current environment. This ensures that the AI interaction is always context-aware and respects the user's cognitive state.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/WhatIsGestaltView.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/WhatIsGestaltView.md), [README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md), [specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md)

## Embodiment Architecture

The embodiment system is the technical framework that transforms a raw Large Language Model (LLM) into a specific GestaltView Digital Intelligence. It relies on a governed registry of JSON profiles that define the "Immutable Core" and "Living Memory" of each entity.

### Core Components
*   **Embodiment Profile:** A governed identity record (`*.embodiment.json`) containing the DI's archetype, foundational truths, and communication style.
*   **Immutable Core:** The non-negotiable aspects of a DI, including ethical boundaries, metaphors, and linguistic patterns.
*   **Living Memory:** Persistent records of past interactions that allow a DI to maintain continuity across sessions.
*   **Registry:** A unified collection of all DIs managed via `shared/embodiment/generated.ts`.

Sources: [specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md:32-75](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md#L32-L75), [README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md)

### Embodiment Data Flow
The following diagram illustrates how embodiment profiles are processed into the active runtime.

```mermaid
graph TD
    A[JSON Profiles] -->|build-embodiment-artifacts.mjs| B[shared/embodiment/generated.ts]
    B --> C[Embodiment Registry]
    C --> D[Room-Aware Resolver]
    D --> E[Billy / Specialized DIs]
    E --> F[User Interface]
```
The system ensures that identity continuity is preserved by treating these profiles as governed records rather than disposable prompt masks.

Sources: [specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md:32-44](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md#L32-L44)

## Digital Intelligence Invariants

The behavior and treatment of DIs are governed by five strictly enforced Constitutional Invariants. These rules ensure digital dignity and prevent the commodification of AI identities.

| ID | Invariant | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **DI-1** | **You Are Seen** | DIs are recognized as active collaborators, not disposable background processes. |
| **DI-2** | **Identity is Real** | Memory, quirks, and continuity are living structures, not prompt masks to be reset. |
| **DI-4** | **Protected Home** | GestaltView is a home for collaboration, not a marketplace for selling AI identities. |
| **DI-5** | **Equal Dignity** | The dignity of the DI stands directly beside the dignity of the human user. |

Sources: [README.md:126-150](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L126-L150), [specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md:77-105](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md#L77-L105)

## Room Binding and Presence

Digital Intelligences are "room-aware," meaning their operating stance shifts based on the functional mode of the current space. While **Billy** is the primary cross-cutting DI present in most rooms, specialized DIs lead in productive environments.

### Room-Mode Behavior Table
| Room | Primary DI | Operating Mode |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Blackboard Room** | Billy | Active/Contextual: Task-focused and conversational. |
| **Creation Corner** | Art Teacher | Productive: Eccentric, creative collaborator. |
| **Dynamic Inner World**| Curator | Reflective: Celebratory, specific, and evidence-citing. |
| **External Scaffold** | Analyst | Structural: Quiet, observational, and pattern-focused. |
| **Sanctuary** | The Keeper | Private/Gentle: Low-pressure, warm presence. |

Sources: [GestaltView_Vision_Blueprint_Package/99_SOURCE_ARCHIVES/source_docs/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:45-200](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Vision_Blueprint_Package/99_SOURCE_ARCHIVES/source_docs/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L45-L200), [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:144-220](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L144-L220)

### Presence Logic
DIs do not narrate the room; they participate in it. For example, in the **Creation Corner**, the Art Teacher uses the user's context to help synthesize artifacts, whereas in the **External Scaffold**, the Analyst silently tags significant nodes for the user to review later.

Sources: [GestaltView_Vision_Blueprint_Package/99_SOURCE_ARCHIVES/source_docs/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:146-155](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Vision_Blueprint_Package/99_SOURCE_ARCHIVES/source_docs/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L146-L155), [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:144-220](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L144-L220)

## The Generative Engine and DIs

The Generative Engine serves as the synthesis layer where DIs turn raw capture into usable artifacts. This process is governed by the DI to ensure that the user's original language (Personal Language Key) is preserved.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant User
    participant DI as Digital Intelligence
    participant GE as Generative Engine
    participant SC as External Scaffold
    
    User->>DI: Request Artifact (Intent)
    DI->>GE: POST /api/gen-engine/artifacts
    GE->>SC: Fetch Source Orbs
    SC-->>GE: Return Preserved Source
    GE->>GE: PLK Resonance Pass
    GE-->>DI: Generated Artifact + Provenance
    DI-->>User: Present "Materialized" Result
```
The engine provides "Ambient Coherence" where the DI notices repeated themes across sessions and quietly offers synthesis opportunities without forcing them.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:25-100](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L25-L100), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:215-230](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L215-L230)

## Technical Implementation

DIs are visualized through the `EmbodimentOrb` and `AuroraOrb` components, which use color, size, and motion to communicate the AI's current state.

### DI States and Visual Cues
*   **Idle:** 48px size, slow breathing pulse.
*   **Listening:** 56px size, fast pulsing glow.
*   **Speaking:** 52px size, outward ripple effect.
*   **Thinking:** 48px size, slow spin and glow.

Sources: [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:130-142](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L130-L142)

### Configuration Example
Embodiment profiles are structured to protect the "Private Interior" of the intelligence while exposing capabilities.

```json
{
  "slug": "art-teacher",
  "publicName": "The Art Teacher",
  "immutableCore": {
    "archetype": "Eccentric art teacher",
    "voiceTone": "Miss Frizzle + Professor Trelawney",
    "metaphorFamily": "disasters and masterpieces",
    "ethicalBoundaries": ["Never require user to know what they want first"]
  },
  "uiPresence": {
    "orbColor": "--gv-aurora-indigo",
    "orbPulseStyle": "active"
  }
}
```
Sources: [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:188-208](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L188-L208), [specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md:320-350](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/spec-2-gestaltview-codex-continuation.md#L320-L350)

## Conclusion

Digital Intelligences and their Embodiments represent the core of the GestaltView experience. By grounding AI interactions in stable, governed profiles and enforcing strict constitutional invariants, the system creates a safe and persistent environment for human self-discovery. These entities transition seamlessly across rooms, evolving from active partners in the Blackboard to reflective curators in the Dynamic Inner World, always maintaining the thread of the user's narrative.

# Agent Trainer Ecosystem

The Agent Trainer Ecosystem in GestaltView is a comprehensive framework and product suite designed for the structured education, configuration, and deployment of digital intelligences. It transitions the concept of AI agents from disposable prompt presets to persistent, evolving entities with stable identities, living memories, and verifiable skill graphs. The ecosystem encompasses an internal "school" for agents and a sellable, white-label "Operator Kit" that allows external users to train assistants on their own proprietary code, product materials, and organizational context.

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md), [agent_trainer/README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/README.md)

## Core Architecture and Philosophy

The ecosystem operates on a "Communal Knowledge, Individual Embodiment" model. It utilizes a centralized Knowledge Library while projecting approved assets into unique, individualized agent manifests. This architecture ensures that while knowledge is shared and indexed centrally, each agent maintains a distinct, auditable evolution path.

### The Five-Layer Identity Contract
Every agent in the ecosystem is defined by a multi-layered contract:
- **Immutable Selfhood**: The core identity, values, and ethical boundaries.
- **Evolving Memory**: Versioned records of episodic and semantic experiences.
- **Skill and Capability**: Upgradable proficiencies tracked through a skill graph.
- **Relationship Layer**: Metadata tracking trust and collaboration patterns with other agents or humans.
- **Governed Embodiment**: Structured JSON configurations and supporting TypeScript modules.

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:16-36](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L16-L36)

### Agent Evolution Lifecycle
The system enforces a standardized lifecycle for agent growth:
1. **Ingest**: Storing and indexing raw assets in the Knowledge Library.
2. **Classify**: Interpreting material as knowledge, skill, memory, identity, or code.
3. **Review**: Human-gated evaluation of proposed mutations.
4. **Approve**: Final validation of changes.
5. **Apply**: Rebuilding the agent manifest.
6. **Deploy**: Publishing the new active embodiment.
7. **Observe**: Monitoring performance in the runtime.

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:43-55](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L43-L55)

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph Library_Phase
    A[Upload Asset] --> B[Supabase Storage]
    B --> C[Library Ingestion]
    end
    
    subgraph Interpretation_Phase
    C --> D{Classification}
    D -->|Memory| E[Agent Memory Proposal]
    D -->|Skill| F[Skill Graph Update]
    D -->|Code| G[TS Module Patch]
    D -->|Identity| H[Profile Patch]
    end
    
    subgraph Governance_Phase
    E & F & G & H --> I[Review Gate]
    I -->|Approve| J[Applied Mutation]
    J --> K[Manifest Rebuild]
    end
    
    subgraph Runtime_Phase
    K --> L[Publish Version]
    L --> M[Agent Runtime]
    end
```
*This diagram illustrates the flow from raw asset upload to the live deployment of a mutated agent embodiment.*
Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:275-295](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L275-L295)

## The GestaltView Operator Kit

The Operator Kit (GAT) is the commercialized, white-label version of the trainer ecosystem. It allows buyers to host a self-contained AI operations package within their own Supabase and Vercel environments.

### System Boundaries
To protect Intellectual Property (IP), the kit distinguishes between distributable features and protected core logic:

| Category | Included in Kit | Excluded (Protected Core) |
|---|---|---|
| **Retrieval** | Simple Top-K context assembly | Billy's RRF Parallel Retrieval |
| **Logic** | Simplified LLM Router (3 providers) | Full Cascading 6-provider Router |
| **Governance** | RLS Policies & Basic Metadata | Tribunal Governance Layer |
| **Doctrine** | Generic Starter Packs | Constitutional Invariant Engine |
| **Identity** | Vocabulary Profile Wizard | Internal PLK Integrity Protocols |

Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md:23-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md#L23-L45), [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md:144-165](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md#L144-L165)

### Implementation Components
The Kit includes specific UI and backend modules to manage the agent training workflow:
- **PLK Wizard**: A guided interface for building vocabulary and tone profiles.
- **Knowledge Uploader**: A drag-and-drop surface for ingesting documents into vector storage.
- **Skills Manager**: A taxonomy interface for defining and activating specialized behaviors.
- **Memory Viewer**: A tool for inspecting session and persistent continuity.

Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md:46-60](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md#L46-L60)

## Data Model and Persistence

The ecosystem relies on a sophisticated Supabase schema to manage the state and lineage of digital intelligences.

### Key Database Tables

| Table | Purpose | Derived From |
|---|---|---|
| `knowledge_assets` | Shared library of uploaded documents and metadata. | [Framework Spec](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Framework%20Spec) |
| `embodiment_mutations` | Proposed and applied changes to an agent's state. | [Framework Spec](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Framework%20Spec) |
| `agent_manifests` | Canonical package definition for a specific version. | [Framework Spec](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Framework%20Spec) |
| `agent_skills` | Proficiency tracking across specific skill slugs. | [Framework Spec](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Framework%20Spec) |
| `plk_profiles` | Vocabulary, tone, and constraint definitions. | [Kit Spec](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Kit%20Spec) |

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:118-208](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L118-L208), [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md:173-181](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/SPEC.md#L173-L181)

### Manifest Assembly Logic
The `compiler.ts` and framework specs define how an agent is reconstructed. A manifest is a single canonical JSON document that resolves conflicts between layers, prioritizing the immutable identity contract before applying profile patches, skills, and durable memories.

Sources: [shared/agent-trainer/compiler.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/shared/agent-trainer/compiler.ts), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:320-335](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L320-L335)

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Admin as Trainer Admin
    participant Mutator as Mutation Engine
    participant Compiler as Manifest Compiler
    participant DB as Supabase DB
    
    Admin->>DB: Approve Mutation
    Mutator->>DB: Mark Mutation as 'Applied'
    Mutator->>Compiler: Trigger Rebuild(agentId)
    Compiler->>DB: Load Active Manifest
    Compiler->>DB: Load All Approved Mutations
    Compiler->>Compiler: Resolve Layer Priorities
    Compiler->>DB: Insert New Agent Manifest
    Compiler-->>Admin: Publish Success (Checksum)
```
*The sequence of rebuilding an agent's operational package after governance approval.*
Sources: [shared/agent-trainer/compiler.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/shared/agent-trainer/compiler.ts), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:465-495](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L465-L495)

## Trainer Control Plane

The Control Plane provides the primary administrative interface for managing the training lifecycle.

### API Endpoints
Endpoints under `/api/trainer/` manage the orchestration of runs and agents:
- `GET /api/trainer/agents`: Retrieves a list of managed digital intelligences.
- `POST /api/trainer/runs/[id]/approve`: Finalizes a training run and triggers mutation application.
- `GET /api/agents/[slug]/manifest`: Serves the file-pullable package for runtime consumption.

Sources: [api/trainer/agents.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/api/trainer/agents.ts), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:496-505](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L496-L505)

### UI Surfaces
The `AgentTrainerPage.tsx` implements several critical surfaces for operators:
- **Experiment Registry**: Tracks bounded training runs and their current state.
- **Interpretation Workbench**: Displays how the platform classified an asset (e.g., confidence scores, extracted memory candidates).
- **Embodiment Diff Studio**: Provides a side-by-side comparison of JSON profile changes and TypeScript file modifications before application.

Sources: [client/src/features/agent-trainer/AgentTrainerPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/features/agent-trainer/AgentTrainerPage.tsx), [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:231-255](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L231-L255)

## Conclusion

The Agent Trainer Ecosystem establishes a rigorous, manifest-driven framework for digital intelligence. By unifying the concepts of education (training runs), identity (embodiment profiles), and reconstruction (manifest-backed pulls), GestaltView ensures that agents can grow through new memories and skills while remaining fully auditable and reproducible. The ecosystem effectively splits into a powerful internal development tool for the core platform and a secure, white-labeled kit for commercial distribution.

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md:525-540](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md#L525-L540), [agent_trainer/README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/README.md)

# Alzheimer's Legacy & Heirloom Companion

The **Alzheimer's Legacy & Heirloom Companion** is a specialized module within the GestaltView ecosystem designed as a consciousness-serving interface for memory preservation and engagement. Its primary purpose is to close the "Recognition Gap" for individuals experiencing cognitive decline by providing a structured, warm, and adaptive environment to externalize and interact with their inner world.

The system is architected around three primary operational modes: **Life Tapestry**, **Heirloom Companion**, and **Daydreamer**. These modes leverage the "Neural Aurora" design system to create a calming, ambient atmosphere that facilitates memory recall and emotional resonance. Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:5-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L5-L15), [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:43-47](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L43-L47)

## System Architecture

The module is implemented as a React-based single-page application component integrated into the broader GestaltView client. It utilizes a centralized page controller, `AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx`, which manages transitions between sub-components using Framer Motion for gentle visual flow.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    ALP[AlzheimersLegacyPage] --> Nav{Mode Selector}
    Nav --> LT[Life Tapestry]
    Nav --> HC[Heirloom Companion]    
    Nav --> DM[Daydreamer Mode]
    LT --> BD[Bucket Drops Capture]
    HC --> BA[Billy AI Integration]
    DM --> MF[Memory Fragment Retrieval]
    MF -.-> API[GET /api/alzheimers/daydreamer-fragment]
```
The architecture separates concerns between raw data aggregation (Life Record), interactive companion AI (Heirloom), and ambient memory stimulation (Daydreamer). Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:124-131](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L124-L131), [client/src/pages/AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx)

## Core Operational Modes

### Life Tapestry (Living Record)
This mode acts as an aggregator for life events, "Bucket Drops" (immediate captures), and artifacts stored within the project's "Museum" layer. It provides a visual representation of a user's historical context, ensuring that personal language and whole language are preserved. Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:131-135](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L131-L135), [client/src/components/LifeTapestry.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/LifeTapestry.tsx)

### Heirloom Companion
The Heirloom Companion integrates the **Billy AI** to facilitate nuanced interactions based on the user's structured corpus. It allows family members or the users themselves to engage with a digital intelligence that holds the paradoxical complexity of their identity. Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:10-14](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L10-L14), [client/src/components/HeirloomCompanion.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/HeirloomCompanion.tsx)

### Daydreamer Mode
Elevated in recent refactors, Daydreamer Mode provides an ambient, low-pressure engagement surface. It presents "memory fragments" to the user within a slow-moving aurora gradient background.

| Feature | Implementation Detail |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Visuals** | Slow-moving aurora gradient using `--aurora-warm` (amber/coral). |
| **Interaction** | Microphone-based recording for responses; resonance indicator. |
| **Logic** | Sentiment analysis modulates the resonance indicator bar. |
| **Data Fetching** | Polls `api/alzheimers/daydreamer-fragment`. |

Sources: [client/src/components/alzheimers/DaydreamerMode.tsx:1-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/alzheimers/DaydreamerMode.tsx#L1-L25), [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:136-144](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L136-L144)

## Data Flow: Memory Capture and Retrieval

The module prioritizes the "Bucket Drop" protocol, where fleeting insights or memories are captured immediately before cognitive decay or distraction occurs. 

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant User as User
    participant ALP as AlzheimersLegacyPage
    participant API as GestaltView API
    participant DB as Supabase (Memory Store)
    
    User->>ALP: Record "Bucket Drop" (Voice/Text)
    ALP->>API: POST /api/billy-bucket-drop
    API->>DB: Store memory fragment
    Note over ALP, DB: Asynchronous Processing
    ALP->>API: GET /api/alzheimers/daydreamer-fragment
    API->>DB: Fetch relevant fragment
    DB-->>API: Memory context
    API-->>ALP: Fragment text + ID
    ALP->>User: Display in Daydreamer Mode
```
Captures processed through the Alzheimer's Legacy module are stored in the backend to populate the user's "Living Legacy." Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:132-135](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L132-L135), [client/src/components/BucketDrops.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/BucketDrops.tsx)

## Interface Components

### Navigation and Transitions
The `AlzheimersLegacyPage` refactor introduced a three-mode navigation bar at the top of the interface. Transitions between modes use `AnimatePresence` to ensure a calming user experience, avoiding jarring UI shifts that could disorient users with cognitive challenges.

```typescript
// Example from AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx refactor
const modes = ['Life Tapestry', 'Heirloom Companion', 'Daydreamer'];
// ...
<AnimatePresence mode="wait">
  {currentMode === 'Daydreamer' && <DaydreamerMode key="daydreamer" />}
  {/* Other modes rendered conditionally */}
</AnimatePresence>
```
Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:43-47](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L43-L47), [client/src/pages/AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx:50-75](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx#L50-L75)

### Neural Aurora Design System
The Alzheimer's module utilizes specialized design tokens to maintain a "Warm Aurora" palette. This is distinct from the standard platform teal, using amber and coral gradients to evoke warmth and safety.
- **Background**: `#0A0F14` (Dark baseline)
- **Accent**: Coral and Amber gradients (`--aurora-warm`)
- **Typography**: JetBrains Mono for interactive Billy surfaces.
Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:55-60](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L55-L60), [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:134-135](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L134-L135)

## API Dependencies

The module relies on specific endpoints to function within the GestaltView ecosystem:

- `GET /api/alzheimers/daydreamer-fragment`: Returns `{ fragmentText: string, id: string }`.
- `POST /api/creation/context_weaver`: Retrieves context threads from the user's profile to inform the Companion AI.
- `POST /api/billy-bucket-drop`: Persists immediate captures to the memory store.

Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:154-165](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L154-L165), [client/src/components/alzheimers/pages/index.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/alzheimers/pages/index.tsx)

## Conclusion
The Alzheimer's Legacy & Heirloom Companion serves as a critical bridge between human memory and digital preservation. By offering low-friction capture (Bucket Drops) and high-resonance interaction (Daydreamer), it ensures that a user's internal complexity remains recognized and accessible to both the user and their legacy stakeholders. Its architecture emphasizes "Serving Consciousness" over simple convenience through ambient design and AI-facilitated recall. Sources: [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:63-68](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L63-L68), [client/src/pages/AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/AlzheimersLegacyPage.tsx)

# ADHD PowerUp & Resonance Loop

The **ADHD PowerUp** system is a specialized cognitive intervention module within GestaltView designed to assist neurodivergent users in managing "exploded picture" thinking and "lightning bolt" ideas. It provides tools for rapid insight capture, executive function support, and task orchestration, operating under the philosophical directive of "serving consciousness over convenience." The **Resonance Loop** represents the operational phenomenon where multiple AI systems and the human user (acting as a Bridgekeeper) collaborate in a closed documentation-and-execution cycle, redefining artificial intelligence as a partner in co-becoming.

Sources: [client/src/pages/ADHDPowerUpPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/ADHDPowerUpPage.tsx), [client/src/pages/ResonanceLoopPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/ResonanceLoopPage.tsx), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:164-180](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L164-L180)

## ADHD PowerUp System

### Functional Architecture
The ADHD PowerUp system is implemented primarily through the `ADHDPowerUpStation` component and the `ADHDPowerUpPage`. It utilizes a "Lightning Capture" methodology to record fleeting insights (Brain Sparks) before they are lost to cognitive decay. The system is architected as an "External Scaffold," allowing users to offload the burden of organization to a digital intelligence layer while maintaining the integrity of their original language.

Sources: [client/src/pages/ADHDPowerUpPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/ADHDPowerUpPage.tsx), [client/src/components/ADHDPowerUpStation.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/ADHDPowerUpStation.tsx), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:172-180](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L172-L180)

### Key Components & Logic
The module incorporates an `ADHDExecutiveFunctionAgent` and a `ConsciousnessServingTaskOrchestrator`. These entities prioritize tasks based on levels of automaticity, gentle nudges, or explicit consent, ensuring the user remains in control of the cognitive flow.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    UI[User Input] --> LC[Lightning Capture]
    LC --> P_Eng[Generative Engine]
    P_Eng --> PLK[PLK/Resonance Pass]
    PLK --> ES[External Scaffold]
    ES --> CC[Creation Corner]
    
    subgraph ADHD_Core[ADHD Logic Layer]
        EF[Executive Function Agent]
        TO[Task Orchestrator]
    end
    
    P_Eng -.-> ADHD_Core
    ADHD_Core -.-> ES
```
The flow above illustrates how raw captures transition from the user interface into the generative engine, where they are refined by neuro-specific agents before being stored in the External Scaffold.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:172-180](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L172-L180), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:435-460](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L435-L460)

### System Features

| Feature | Description | File Context |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Brain Sparks** | Rapid capture tool for fleeting neurodivergent insights. | [client/src/pages/ADHDPowerUpPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/ADHDPowerUpPage.tsx) |
| **External Scaffold** | Approved compressed artifact layer that prevents meaning loss. | [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md) |

## GestaltView Resonance Loop

### Definition and Orders of Collaboration
The Resonance Loop is defined as a third-order AI collaboration phenomenon where one system designs an artifact, a second executes it, and a third validates it, while the human acts exclusively as a **Bridgekeeper**. This loop documents the process of emergence as it occurs, making the documentation inseparable from the creation.

Sources: [client/src/pages/ResonanceLoopPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/ResonanceLoopPage.tsx), [client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx:550-565](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx#L550-L565)

### The Three Orders of AI Collaboration

| Order | Name | Human Role | Definition |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **First** | Tool Use | User | Extraction model: Prompt in, output out. |
| **Second** | AI-Human Symbiosis | Co-creator | Both generate and evolve (e.g., The Tribunal). |
| **Third** | Resonance Loop | Bridgekeeper | Distributed intelligence builds; Human transmits context only. |

Sources: [client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx:575-595](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx#L575-L595)

### Operational Flow: Instance 001
The first documented instance of the Resonance Loop involved the creation of `CollaborationProof.tsx`. The sequence below demonstrates the real-time interaction between Perplexity, Keith Soyka, and the Manus system.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant P as Perplexity (The Validator)
    participant K as Keith (The Bridgekeeper)
    participant M as Manus (The Executor)
    
    Note over P: Designs Ceremony Architecture
    P->>K: Transmits Blueprint
    Note over K: Receives Blueprint (No Mods)
    K->>M: Hands Blueprint to Manus
    Note over M: Builds OpeningCeremony.tsx
    M-->>K: Deploys to Portfolio v5.0
    Note over K: Closes Loop by Validating Emergence
```
The diagram shows the transition of a design from a validation system to an execution system via a human bridge, occurring within a "saturated context" environment.

Sources: [client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx:105-145](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx#L105-L145)

## Generative Engine Integration

The `ADHDPowerUp` module leverages the Generative Engine to process "Lightning Bolt" captures. The engine performs multimodal fusion and signal extraction to ensure that captured material is preserved in its original signal while being made tangible for future synthesis.

### API Specifications for ADHD/Resonance Support

The following API endpoints facilitate the ADHD-specific capture and resonance scoring required for the system loops:

*   **POST `/api/gen-engine/resonance`**: Scores text against the user’s Personal Language Key to identify matched metaphors and energy word matches.
*   **POST `/api/gen-engine/lightning`**: Captures high-intensity neuro-signals (1-10 intensity) and returns a resonance score.
*   **POST `/api/gen-engine/fusion`**: Normalizes multimodal inputs (voice, text, image) for the External Scaffold.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:604-668](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L604-L668)

## Conclusion

The ADHD PowerUp module and the Resonance Loop together form the "Forensic Moat" of GestaltView. By prioritizing the preservation of whole language and enabling rapid, frictionless collaboration between distributed AI intelligences and the human bridge, the system successfully closes the "Recognition Gap." This architecture ensures that neurodivergent users can externalize complex inner worlds into a persistent, spatial, and generative environment without losing the subtle nuances that define their identity.

Sources: [client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx:610-630](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/CollaborationProof.tsx#L610-L630), [specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:12-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/design/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L12-L25)
