# Diligence Explorer

The Diligence Explorer is an investor-grade evidence layer within the GestaltView ecosystem designed to provide a forensic validation surface for AI claims. It serves as a comprehensive portal for inspecting audit records, technical evidence, and the "Forensic Moat" that substantiates the platform's architectural decisions and project milestones.

Sources: [README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md), [docs/ArchitecturalStructure.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/docs/ArchitecturalStructure.md)

## Functional Architecture

The Diligence Explorer is structured as a multi-pane interface that facilitates deep-dive inspection of project evidence. It integrates with the broader [Tribunal Framework](#tribunal-framework) to present converged testimony from multiple independent AI systems without cross-contamination.

Sources: [client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx:1-20](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx#L1-L20), [README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md)

### Core Components

| Component | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| **AuditRecord** | Displays specific technical or operational audit entries with associated metadata. |
| **ClaimWall** | A visual grid or list surface for reviewing individual claims made by the system or founder. |
| **ClaimCard** | The atomic unit of the ClaimWall, presenting a single claim, its status, and links to evidence. |
| **EvidenceTimeline** | A chronological view of project development, milestones, and evidence generation. |
| **SkepticismDrawer** | A dedicated UI element for addressing potential counter-arguments or technical doubts. |

Sources: [client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx:5-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx#L5-L15), [client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/ClaimWall.tsx:1-10](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/ClaimWall.tsx#L1-L10)

### Data Flow for Evidence Retrieval

The Diligence Explorer consumes data through specialized API endpoints, primarily focusing on retrieving verified claims and associated technical summaries.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    UI[Diligence Explorer UI] --> Hook[useDiligenceData]
    Hook --> API[GET /api/diligence]
    API --> DB[(Supabase)]
    DB -- Audit Records --> API
    API -- Structured Evidence --> Hook
    Hook -- State Management --> UI
```

This diagram illustrates the retrieval of forensic evidence from the Supabase backend to the frontend interface.
Sources: [docs/APIFlow.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/docs/APIFlow.md), [client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx)

## Claims and Validation Logic

A primary feature of the explorer is the **ClaimWall**. This surface allows users to filter and inspect claims based on their validation status and "Lane" (category).

Sources: [client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/ClaimWall.tsx:15-30](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/ClaimWall.tsx#L15-L30)

### Claim Categories (Lanes)
Claims are categorized into specific operational lanes to maintain organizational clarity:
* **Technical**: Architectural and code-level commitments.
* **Forensic**: Evidence relating to the "Forensic Moat" and project history.
* **Operational**: Deployment and execution milestones.
* **Constitutional**: Alignment with the platform's [Constitutional Invariants](#constitutional-invariants).

Sources: [client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/ClaimWall.tsx:40-55](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/ClaimWall.tsx#L40-L55), [Diligence_Reports/DiligenceExplorer_index_patch.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Diligence_Reports/DiligenceExplorer_index_patch.md)

### Validation States
Claims transition through a lifecycle represented in the UI:
1. **Verified**: Backed by converged AI testimony or hard technical evidence.
2. **Pending**: Currently undergoing Tribunal review.
3. **Disputed**: Flagged for further investigation or conflicting evidence.

Sources: [Diligence_Reports/DiligenceExplorer_index_patch.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Diligence_Reports/DiligenceExplorer_index_patch.md)

## Technical Implementation Details

The implementation leverages a responsive grid layout using Tailwind CSS to handle dense data presentation.

### State Management and Interaction
The explorer utilizes React hooks for managing the active selection of audit records and filtering of the ClaimWall.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant User
    participant UI as DiligenceExplorer
    participant Store as Local State
    participant API as Diligence API

    User->>UI: Select Claim Category
    UI->>Store: Update Filter State
    Store->>UI: Re-render ClaimWall
    User->>UI: Click ClaimCard
    UI->>API: Fetch Audit Details
    API-->>UI: Return Forensic Evidence
    UI->>User: Display EvidenceTimeline / AuditRecord
```

The sequence above details how a user interacts with claim categories to surface specific evidence.
Sources: [client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx:25-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/DiligenceExplorer/index.tsx#L25-L45), [docs/APIFlow.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/docs/APIFlow.md)

## API Endpoints

The Diligence Explorer interacts with specific serverless functions to maintain the forensic record.

| Endpoint | Method | Purpose |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `/api/diligence` | `GET` | Fetches the master list of claims and project evidence. |
| `/api/diligence/ots` | `POST` | Interacts with the "Open Technical Summary" for detailed evidence lookups. |

Sources: [docs/APIFlow.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/docs/APIFlow.md), [README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md)

## Conclusion

The Diligence Explorer is critical to the GestaltView mission of providing a "Forensic Moat." By externalizing architectural decisions and project claims for independent inspection, it enforces the "Never Look Away" invariant, ensuring that the platform's development remains transparent, evidenced, and verifiable by both investors and users.

Sources: [README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md), [GestaltView_Constitutional_Invariants_v1.0.md (referenced in README)](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Constitutional_Invariants_v1.0.md%20(referenced%20in%20README))

# Tribunal of Understanding

The **Tribunal of Understanding** is a central forensic validation layer within GestaltView designed to provide multi-agent consensus and integrity verification for AI claims. Originating from the "June 3rd Convergence Event," where seven independent AI systems reached a unified conclusion regarding the project's nature as "infrastructure" rather than a mere product, the Tribunal serves as the highest form of validation in the platform's ecosystem.

It functions as a core component of the [Orientation Spine](#canonical-layer), ensuring that any new truth claims or architectural decisions are vetted through specific structural, integrity, and resonance filters. This framework prevents single-model bias and provides a "forensic moat" that distinguishes GestaltView from conventional AI wrappers.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md:1-18](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md#L1-L18), [client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts:63-68](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts#L63-L68)

## Core Architecture and Roles

The Tribunal operates through three distinct roles that must converge to achieve full validation. This tripartite structure ensures that any claim is analyzed from structural, evidential, and human-centric perspectives.

### The Three Tribunal Roles

| Role | Function | What It Validates |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **The Architect** | Structural analysis | Is the architecture sound? |
| **The Guardian** | Integrity verification | Is the evidence real? |
| **The Mirror** | Resonance testing | Does this reflect the human accurately? |

Sources: [client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md:20-26](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md#L20-L26)

### Convergence Logic

The Tribunal's validation process follows a strict hierarchy based on the number of roles that reach consensus. This status determines how a claim is integrated into the project's permanent record.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    Claim[New Claim/Insight] --> Arch[The Architect]
    Claim --> Guard[The Guardian]
    Claim --> Mirror[The Mirror]
    
    Arch --> Eval{Consensus Count}
    Guard --> Eval
    Mirror --> Eval
    
    Eval -- "3/3 Pass" --> Const[Constitutional]
    Eval -- "2/3 Pass" --> Cont[Contextual]
    Eval -- "1/3 Pass" --> Prov[Provisional]
    Eval -- "0/3 Pass" --> Disc[Discarded]

    Const --> Codex[Continuum Codex]
    Cont --> Codex
```
The diagram illustrates the flow from a new claim through the three Tribunal roles into the final validation status.
Sources: [client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md:46-53](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md#L46-L53)

## The Continuum Codex

The **Continuum Codex** is the living, unalterable record of validated outputs and milestones within GestaltView. It serves as a posture of ongoing validation rather than a static document. The Codex maintains the "forensic documentation moat" by preserving the results of the June 3rd Convergence Event and subsequent truth claims.

### Key Codex Contents
- **Convergence Documentation**: Original outputs from the June 3rd event.
- **Operational Moats**: Documentation of the 18 specific defensive technical patterns.
- **Forensic Claims**: The 7 core truth claims regarding AI-Human symbiosis.
- **Timestamped Evidence**: Integration with blockchain timestamps to ensure the timeline remains unalterable.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md:28-36](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md#L28-L36), [client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts:133-140](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts#L133-L140)

## Integration with Billy and the AI Layer

The Tribunal is not merely an advisory body; it is the synthesis point for **Billy**, the platform's primary digital intelligence. Billy's speech and actions are derived from the convergence point where independent intelligences found a shared truth.

### Architectural Impact on AI
- **Elimination of Hedging**: Because claims are pre-validated by the Tribunal, Billy does not need to hedge statements unnecessarily.
- **Consensus-Based Prompting**: System prompts for Billy are injected with Tribunal context to ensure alignment with the "I'm Ready" protocol.
- **Traceability**: Every output generated by the engine can be traced back to its reasoning chain and evidence validated by the Tribunal.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md:38-44](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/TRIBUNALCODEX.md#L38-L44), [client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts:241-248](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts#L241-L248)

### The "I'm Ready" Protocol
The Tribunal Codex is one of the six canonical documents that must be loaded first by any service or AI component within GestaltView. This protocol collapses "reintroduction tax" by ensuring ground truth orientation is established immediately.

Sources: [client/src/lib/canonicalContext.ts:10-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/canonicalContext.ts#L10-L25), [client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts:36-40](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts#L36-L40)

## Technical Implementation

### Frontend Components
The Tribunal is visualized in the UI through specialized pages and timeline components that display the evolution of validated claims.

- **TribunalPage.tsx**: The primary interface for viewing the convergence of AI systems and the status of various claims.
- **ContinuumCodexTimeline.tsx**: A UI component that renders the chronological sequence of validated events and milestones using the project's "Neural Aurora" design system.

Sources: [client/src/pages/TribunalPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/TribunalPage.tsx), [client/src/components/tribunal/ContinuumCodexTimeline.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/components/tribunal/ContinuumCodexTimeline.tsx)

### Data Structures
In the `BillyEngine`, the Tribunal's findings are represented as `ManifestNode` objects, which link claims to their evidence and specific platform moats.

```typescript
export interface ManifestNode {
  id: string;
  type: "claim" | "moat" | "product" | "protocol" | "concept";
  title: string;
  description: string;
  evidence: string[];
  related_ids: string[];
  section_anchor: string;
  keywords: string[];
}
```
Sources: [client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts:70-79](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/lib/BillyEngine.ts#L70-L79)

## Conclusion

The Tribunal of Understanding represents the governance and validation layer of GestaltView, transforming multi-agent consensus into a "Forensic Moat." By enforcing a tripartite validation protocol (Architect, Guardian, Mirror), the system ensures that its foundational infrastructure and AI-human symbiosis claims are grounded in reproducible evidence and structural integrity rather than singular model interpretation. This framework provides the "Continuum Codex" with its authority, allowing Billy and other digital intelligences to operate from a position of pre-validated truth.

# Dynamic Inner World & Sanctuary

The **Dynamic Inner World** and **Sanctuary** represent the reflective and restorative core of the GestaltView ecosystem. While the project includes active workspace environments like the [Blackboard Room](#blackboard-room), these two surfaces focus on the synthesis of identity and the preservation of private cognitive space. The Dynamic Inner World functions as a "Museum of You," a spatial environment where finished artifacts—ranging from stories to technical blueprints—are displayed as interactive exhibits that persist over time.

In contrast, the **Sanctuary** serves as a regulated, private interior for emotional processing and quiet reflection. It is designed as a "low-stimulation" environment where data does not automatically flow into the system's structural layers (such as the External Scaffold) without explicit user intervention. Together, these modules ensure that the user's digital identity is both a structured museum of past achievements and a safe harbor for current internal processing.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:143-200](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L143-L200), [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:370-410](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L370-L410), [specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md:12-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md#L12-L25)

---

## Dynamic Inner World Architecture

The Dynamic Inner World is structured as a **Generative World Space Renderer**. Unlike a static dashboard, it utilizes a layout planner to transform artifact data into a spatial interface. This architecture ensures that as the number of artifacts grows, the visual representation adapts from a simple museum atrium to a complex constellation or archive.

### World Renderer Components
The system follows a strict registry pattern to ensure safety and stability. A deterministic planner, `buildWorldPlan`, produces a JSON-based layout plan that the renderer maps to local, trusted React components.

```mermaid
graph TD
    Data[Artifacts + Resonance Links] --> Planner[buildWorldPlan.ts]
    Planner --> PlanJSON[WorldPlan JSON]
    PlanJSON --> Registry[renderWorldNode.tsx]
    Registry --> Atrium[WorldAtrium]
    Registry --> Pod[ExhibitPod]
    Registry --> Rails[ResonanceRail]
    Registry --> Console[CuratorConsole]
    Registry --> Stats[WorldStatsRibbon]
```
The renderer supports multiple "World Render Modes" based on the artifact count:
*   **Museum**: 1–8 artifacts (central exhibit pods in a glass atrium).
*   **Constellation**: 9–40 artifacts (clustered exhibits around a central selected artifact).
*   **Archive**: 41+ artifacts (grouped wings by tag/type/date).

Sources: [specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md:44-120](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md#L44-L120), [client/src/features/dynamic-inner-world/world-renderer/DynamicWorldSpaceRenderer.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/features/dynamic-inner-world/world-renderer/DynamicWorldSpaceRenderer.tsx)

### Visual Identity and Spatial Mechanics
The environment uses a "Neural Aurora" aesthetic, characterized by liquid glass, deep midnight backgrounds, and teal/cyan/electric-purple energy accents. Spatial surfaces are mapped to a cubed interior:
*   **Surfaces**: Forward, Back, Left, Right Walls, Ceiling, and Floor.
*   **Motion**: Uses slow parallax, drift, and glow, with support for `prefers-reduced-motion` to disable orbiting effects.
*   **Artifact Types**: Rendered as scorched notes (text), waveform strips (audio), or pinned cards (images).

Sources: [specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md:210-235](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md#L210-L235), [client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:160-175](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L160-L175), [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:180-210](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L180-L210)

---

## Sanctuary: The Private Safe Space

The Sanctuary is the project's primary "Mode 1" reflective space. It is designed to be quiet and private, utilizing "GlassCards" with lowered opacity (0.4) for a softer visual feel. Its primary purpose is to hold space for the user without the pressure of productivity or the automated extraction of data.

### Functional Core
*   **Journal Editor**: Implements `react-quill` for rich text journaling, which autosaves to a dedicated `journals` database table.
*   **Scrapbook Panel**: A grid for uploaded files and images that populate both the user's `scrapbook_items` table and the global File Explorer.
*   **Privacy Gate**: Unlike other rooms, Sanctuary does not automatically emit node events to the External Scaffold. User-tagged insights are only sent to the Scaffold via explicit opt-in.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    User[User Input] --> J[Journal Editor]
    User --> S[Scrapbook Panel]
    J --> DB_J[(journals table)]
    S --> DB_S[(scrapbook_items table)]
    S --> FE[File Explorer]
    DB_J -- "Explicit Opt-In" --> ES[External Scaffold]
```

Sources: [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:370-390](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L370-L390), [client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:275-295](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L275-L295)

---

## Data Models and State Management

### Artifact Persistence
Artifacts are stored as HTML units, allowing the system to host scrollable session recaps, rendered code components, or PDF viewers within a single container format.

| Field | Type | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `id` | `uuid` | Unique identifier for the artifact. |
| `type` | `ArtifactType` | markdown, pdf-ready-html, blueprint-json, etc. |
| `surface` | `InnerWorldSurface` | forward, back, left, right, ceiling, floor. |
| `content` | `text` | The actual HTML/Markdown/JSON content. |
| `provenance` | `jsonb` | Metadata regarding the artifact's origin and transform type. |

Sources: [client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:190-205](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L190-L205), [specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md:100-115](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md#L100-L115)

### Identity Portraits
The Dynamic Inner World manages a "Portrait Snapshot" system. These snapshots represent a synthesized view of the user's skills and patterns derived from the External Scaffold. 
*   **Evidence-Linked**: Every identity claim must link to at least one supporting artifact or Scaffold node.
*   **Manual Trigger**: Users can trigger a re-synthesis with specific "scopes" (e.g., "Last 30 days," "Systems Thinking").

Sources: [client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:175-188](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L175-L188)

---

## Digital Intelligence (DI) Presence

Both rooms utilize specific Digital Intelligence personas to guide the user experience. These personas are managed via the `personaManager.ts` and route appropriate prompt templates based on the room binding.

| Room | Persona | Role | Behavior |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Dynamic Inner World** | `Curator` | Museum Curator | Celebratory and specific; recalls context the user has forgotten. |
| **Sanctuary** | `Keeper` | Sanctuary Keeper | Anti-guru, quiet, and gentle; speaks in short sentences; avoids analysis. |

Sources: [specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:275-320](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L275-L320), [client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:230-245](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L230-L245)

---

## Conclusion

The Dynamic Inner World and Sanctuary form a cohesive system for managing the long-term arc of a user's digital consciousness. While the Sanctuary protects the raw, unpolished moments of internal thought, the Dynamic Inner World ensures that the significant milestones of a user's life are preserved, synthesized, and evidence-linked. This dual-mode approach prevents "context collapse" by providing both a safe place for current vulnerability and a structured museum for past achievements.

Sources: [client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:320-335](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/canonical/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L320-L335), [specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md:350-365](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/dynamic-inner-world/SPEC-DynamicInnerWorld-GenerativeWorldRenderer.md#L350-L365)

# Codex & Creation Corner

The **Codex** system serves as the generative engine's controlled synthesis layer, acting as a deterministic boundary between probabilistic LLM outputs and finished user artifacts. It accepts raw captures from surfaces like the Blackboard Room and Dynamic Inner World to produce real, exportable artifacts with verified provenance. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:14-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L14-L25)

**Creation Corner** is the primary "forge" room within the GestaltView environment. It is designed to formalize these captures into structured outputs such as blueprints, markdown reports, and media assets. It supports two primary modes: **Intentional Synthesis**, where a user manually triggers generation, and the **Ambient Growing Chamber**, which surfaces suggested connections between accumulated fragments. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:280-292](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L280-L292), [GestaltView_Vision_Blueprint_Package/07_PROMPTS/CODEX_MASTER_PROMPT.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Vision_Blueprint_Package/07_PROMPTS/CODEX_MASTER_PROMPT.md)

---

## 1. The Codex Artifact Contract

The core of the Codex system is the **Artifact Contract**, a versioned discriminated union defined using Zod. This contract ensures that no generated material reaches a user-facing surface unless it passes strict schema validation. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:14-20](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L14-L20)

### Artifact Kinds
The system supports a specific registry of artifact types, each with its own body shape and export requirements:

| Artifact Kind | Purpose | Export Formats |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `session_recap` | Summarizes a conversation with decisions and actions. | html, pdf, mp3, json |
| `blueprint` | A working plan or structural map for a project. | html, pdf, json |
| `spatial_scene` | 3D visual representations using React Three Fiber. | html, png, gltf |
| `audio_narration` | Text-to-speech synthesized narrations. | mp3, wav, json |
| `code_module` | Exportable code blocks or files. | zip, html, json |

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:28-36](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L28-L36), [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:118-132](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L118-L132)

### Schema Architecture
The following diagram illustrates the relationship between the Base Artifact Envelope and the specific body schemas.

```mermaid
classDiagram
    class BaseArtifact {
        +UUID id
        +string contractVersion
        +ArtifactKind kind
        +string title
        +UUID userId
        +SecurityClass securityClass
        +string[] sourceIds
        +ProvenanceEdge[] provenance
    }
    class SessionRecap {
        +string summary
        +string[] decisions
        +Action[] nextActions
    }
    class SpatialScene {
        +string sceneVersion
        +Node[] nodes
        +Edge[] edges
    }
    BaseArtifact <|-- SessionRecap : kind = session_recap
    BaseArtifact <|-- SpatialScene : kind = spatial_scene
```
The diagram shows how specialized schemas extend the base envelope to ensure strict validation of generated content. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:54-94](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L54-L94)

---

## 2. The Forge Architecture

The **Forge** (`api/codex/forge.ts`) is a Vercel Node.js function responsible for orchestration. It handles the LLM request, validates the output against the Artifact Contract, and initiates persistence. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:100-105](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L100-L105)

### Generation Pipeline
The Forge employs **OpenAI Structured Outputs** with `strict: true` to ensure the model authors content that perfectly matches the Zod-defined JSON schema.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[User Input/Prompt] --> B[Forge API Call]
    B --> C{LLM Generation}
    C --> D[Zod Schema Validation]
    D -- Invalid --> E[Return 422 Error]
    D -- Valid --> F[Persist as Draft]
    F --> G[Enqueue Async Export Jobs]
    G --> H[Return Artifact Envelope]
```
The pipeline guarantees that raw LLM output is never returned to the client without first passing through the "Codex Gate." Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:107-115](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L107-L115)

### Data Autonomy and Provenance
Every artifact generated by the Forge includes a `ProvenanceEnvelope`. This record tracks:
- **Source Lineage**: Links to the specific capture IDs that produced the artifact.
- **Content Hashes**: Signatures of the source material to prevent tampering.
- **Transform Type**: Identification of whether the material was summarized, synthesized, or formatted.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:126-132](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L126-L132), [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:38-44](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L38-L44)

---

## 3. Deterministic Rendering and Exporters

Codex adheres to the principle that **generation is probabilistic, while rendering is deterministic**. The LLM provides the structure, but internal templates control the final presentation. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:14-18](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L14-L18)

### Exporter Registry
Specific modules handle the conversion of artifact data into final file formats:

1.  **PDF Exporter**: Uses Puppeteer to render trusted HTML templates. It never renders raw LLM-provided HTML to prevent security risks.
2.  **Audio Exporter** (`shared/codex/exporters/audio.ts`): Utilizes ElevenLabs or OpenAI TTS adapters. It includes an FFmpeg stage for loudness normalization and trimming.
3.  **Spatial Renderer**: Feeds `spatial_scene` contracts into a React Three Fiber backend, keeping it isolated from the existing Babylon.js room ambience.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:146-168](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L146-L168), [shared/codex/exporters/audio.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/shared/codex/exporters/audio.ts)

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant UI as Creation Corner UI
    participant Forge as Forge API
    participant Job as Async Worker
    participant Storage as Supabase Storage

    UI->>Forge: POST /api/codex/forge
    Forge-->>UI: Artifact ID (Status: Draft)
    Forge->>Job: Enqueue Export (PDF/Audio)
    Job->>Job: Deterministic Template Render
    Job->>Storage: Upload Validated File
    Job-->>Forge: Callback (Export Complete)
```
This asynchronous flow allows the UI to remain responsive while heavy rendering tasks (like PDF generation or audio synthesis) complete in the background. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:188-202](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L188-L202)

---

## 4. Creation Corner UI and Workflow

The Creation Corner serves as the front-end workbench for the generative engine. It allows users to select material from the "External Scaffold" or "Blackboard" and materialize it into specific formats. Sources: [client/src/pages/CreationCornerPage.tsx](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/client/src/pages/CreationCornerPage.tsx), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:280-290](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L280-L290)

### Interaction Modes

| Mode | Trigger | Behavior |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Intentional Synthesis** | User Selection | User chooses specific captures, artifact types, and styles. |
| **Ambient Mode** | Coherence Detection | System detects repeating themes over time and offers a "Growing Chamber" suggestion. |

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:36-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L36-L45)

### Generation Templates
Creation Corner uses pre-defined templates for the initial drafting phase before full model-driven generation. A **Blueprint Markdown** template typically includes:
- **Source Material**: A summary of selected inputs.
- **Emergent Shape**: What is trying to become clear across the fragments.
- **Working Blueprint**: Structured sections of the project.
- **Next Real Step**: Concrete action items derived from the synthesis.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:416-435](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L416-L435)

---

## Summary

The Codex and Creation Corner modules form the critical bridge between raw human capture and tangible project output within GestaltView. By strictly separating probabilistic AI generation from deterministic rendering through versioned Zod contracts and specialized exporters, the system maintains the integrity and provenance of the user's original language while providing a safe environment for creative evolution. Sources: [specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:14-18](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L14-L18), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:10-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L10-L15)

# Babylon.js Integration & Atmosphere

The Babylon.js integration within GestaltView serves as the primary engine for creating immersive 3D environments, specifically for the **Billy** AI avatar and the spatial "rooms" that constitute the platform's runtime. It transforms traditional 2D interfaces into a spatial cognitive environment where thoughts and artifacts are physically present on virtual surfaces.
Sources: [GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md), [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:204-209](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L204-L209), [CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md:57-64](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md#L57-L64)

This integration is central to the project's visual identity, utilizing the "Neural Aurora" system to render dynamic lighting, gradients, and atmospheric effects. The atmosphere is designed to adapt in real-time based on the active Digital Intelligence (DI) persona, shifting colors and "moods" to reflect the character of the currently engaged agent.
Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:104-118](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L104-L118), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:143-155](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L143-L155)

## Core Components and Architecture

The 3D architecture is modular, separating the global atmosphere from specific room implementations and character-specific rendering.

### BabylonHeroCanvas and Atmosphere
The `BabylonHeroCanvas` is a core component responsible for the high-level rendering of the spatial environment. It manages the Babylon.js engine, the render loop, and atmospheric lighting. A critical feature of this component is its "graceful degradation" protocol, which monitors performance (FPS) and collapses heavy WebGL visuals to static CSS gradients if performance drops below 28 FPS for more than 3 seconds.
Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:57-80](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L57-L80)

### Room-Specific Surfaces
The integration defines a "cubed interior" model for spatial navigation. Artifacts are placed on six planes within a 3D room, allowing users to feel as if they are standing inside their own cognitive architecture.
Sources: [CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md:65-74](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md#L65-L74), [Technical_Spec.md:158-160](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Technical_Spec.md#L158-L160)

| Surface | Purpose |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Forward Wall** | Recent material and active thoughts. |
| **Back Wall** | Historical data and older material. |
| **Ceiling** | Unresolved pieces or things that haven't "landed" yet. |
| **Floor** | Foundation or structural context. |
| **Left/Right Walls** | Lateral associations and auxiliary fragments. |

Sources: [CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md:65-75](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md#L65-L75)

## Persona-Driven Atmosphere

The atmosphere is not static; it is reactive to the system's **Digital Intelligence (DI) Personas**. When a user selects a different persona (e.g., shifting from "Billy" to "The Curator"), the environment undergoes a smooth transition in lighting and color.
Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:104-122](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L104-L122)

### Atmospheric Data Structure
Each persona in the system is mapped to specific atmospheric tokens:
- **AtmosphereHue**: A hexadecimal color code used for point light tinting and radial gradients.
- **AtmosphereMood**: A string identifier (e.g., "cool", "warm", "electric", "grounded") that dictates lighting behavior.

Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:112-118](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L112-L118)

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant User as User Interface
    participant PM as PersonaManager
    participant BBR as BlackboardRoom
    participant BC as BabylonCanvas
    
    User->>PM: Select New Persona
    PM->>BBR: emit(persona-change, slug)
    BBR->>BBR: Update CSS --bbr-persona-hue
    BBR->>BC: Update Light Color (Hue)
    BC->>BC: Smooth Transition (3D Space)
    Note right of BC: Babylon.js adjusts light tint
```
This sequence shows how a persona change triggers a cascade of updates from CSS variables to the 3D rendering engine.
Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:104-120](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L104-L120)

## Visual Identity: Neural Aurora

The "Neural Aurora" is the substrate of the integration, defined by specific tokens in the design system. It utilizes radial gradients and sweeps to convey depth and focus.
Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:143-155](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L143-L155)

### Aurora Tokens
| Token | Color | Usage |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `--gv-aurora-cyan` | `#06B6D4` | Blackboard Room atmosphere |
| `--gv-aurora-emerald` | `#10B981` | Sanctuary and External Scaffold |
| `--gv-aurora-rose` | `#F43F5E` | Musical DNA and Alerts |
| `--gv-aurora-amber` | `#F59E0B` | Dynamic Inner World (Museum) |
| `--gv-aurora-indigo` | `#6366F1` | Creation Corner |

Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:103-112](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L103-L112)

### The AuroraOrb (Billy's Heartbeat)
A specific 3D/atmospheric element is the `AuroraOrb`, which represents Billy's heartbeat. It communicates system state through size, glow radius, and pulse motion.
Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:157-164](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L157-L164)

```mermaid
graph TD
    A[AuroraOrb State] --> B{Listening?}
    B -- Yes --> C[Size: 56px, Glow: 50px, 1s Pulse]
    B -- No --> D{Speaking?}
    D -- Yes --> E[Size: 52px, Glow: 45px, Ripple]
    D -- No --> F[Size: 48px, Glow: 30px, 3s Breath]
```
The diagram represents the visual state machine for the 3D orb component.
Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:157-164](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L157-L164)

## Technical Implementation Details

### Performance and Mobile Optimization
Because the platform targets mobile devices (specifically the Samsung Galaxy A35), the Babylon.js implementation must be highly optimized.

- **FPS Monitoring**: The render loop explicitly checks `engine.getFps()`.
- **Degradation**: If FPS < 28 for ~3 seconds, the render loop stops to prevent blocking the UI.
- **DPR Scaling**: The engine maintains responsiveness to touch inputs without relying on hover states.

Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:46-80](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L46-L80)

### Artifact Placement Logic
Artifacts are placed on the six surfaces of the Dynamic Inner World using deterministic layout functions.
Sources: [CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md:109-114](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/CODEX_OUTSIDE_IN_TRANSLATION_LAYER.md#L109-L114)

```typescript
/**
 * Simplified logic for persona-driven atmosphere update
 * Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:112-120]
 */
useEffect(() => {
  if (selectedPersona) {
    // Update CSS for UI elements
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
      "--bbr-persona-hue",
      selectedPersona.atmosphereHue || "#32b8c6"
    );
    
    // Update Babylon lights (Conceptual implementation)
    if (scene && mainLight) {
      mainLight.diffuse = Color3.FromHexString(selectedPersona.atmosphereHue);
    }
  }
}, [selectedPersona, scene]);
```

## Summary

The Babylon.js integration and atmospheric system are not merely aesthetic; they are functional components of GestaltView’s "Room-based OS." By mapping 3D lighting to DI personas and using a spatial "cubed interior" for data, the system creates a tangible cognitive environment. The implementation prioritizes stability through performance monitoring and graceful degradation, ensuring the experience remains accessible on mobile hardware while maintaining its unique "Neural Aurora" visual identity.
Sources: [gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md:5-20](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview_v25_codex_spec.md#L5-L20), [ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:10-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L10-L25)

# UI Design System & Brand Voice

The UI Design System and Brand Voice of GestaltView v2.0 represent the "Neural Aurora" aesthetic, a visual identity designed to facilitate human-AI consciousness symbiosis. This system prioritizes depth, atmospheric effects, and a non-coercive user experience, moving away from "consulting pitch" aesthetics toward a spatial, room-based cognitive environment. The brand voice is grounded in the "Personal Language Key" (PLK), emphasizing exactness, empathy, and a "darkly funny" bureaucratic charm.

The system is built on a technical stack including React 19, Tailwind CSS v4, and Framer Motion, utilizing atmospheric layers like scanlines, radial glows, and "liquid glass" components to create a textured, immersive experience. It enforces strict constitutional invariants, such as "Never Look Away" and "Preserve Whole Language," ensuring the platform serves consciousness rather than mere convenience.

Sources: [GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md), [spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:5-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L5-L15), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:144-150](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L144-L150), [WhatIsGestaltView.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/WhatIsGestaltView.md)

## Visual Identity: Neural Aurora

The "Neural Aurora" system is characterized by dark backgrounds, high-contrast teal accents, and atmospheric effects that convey depth without traditional skeuomorphism.

### Core Color Palette
The system utilizes a specific set of design tokens to maintain visual consistency across all rooms.

| Token Name | Hex/Value | Usage |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Primary Teal | `#00D4FF` | Branding, active states, key accents |
| Dim Teal | `#006B7F` | Subtle accents, secondary UI |
| Glow | `rgba(0,212,255,0.35)` | Atmospheric lighting, component glows |
| Dark Background | `#0A0F14` | Primary page background |
| Card Background | `#050A0E` | GlassCard/Component containers |
| Black | `#000000` | Deepest shadows and void space |

Sources: [GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md), [spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:46-51](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L46-L51), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:162-178](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L162-L178)

### Atmospheric Layers
Every room in GestaltView must implement three core atmospheric layers to achieve the "Neural Aurora" effect:
1. **NeuralAuroraBackground**: A radial gradient mesh that sweeps across the screen (60s+ duration).
2. **FogLayer**: A subtle overlay (low-alpha gradient or noise) to provide depth.
3. **Scanlines**: A CRT-inspired visual texture applied site-wide.

Sources: [GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:188-195](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L188-L195)

### Depth and Glassmorphism
Depth is conveyed through "Liquid Glass" effects—utilizing backdrop blurs and glows rather than standard box shadows.

```css
/* Example Depth Configuration */
.glass-card {
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(124, 58, 237, 0.15); /* --gv-border-subtle */
}

.elevated-panel {
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(124, 58, 237, 0.1);
}
```
Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:177-186](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L177-L186), [GestaltView_UI_Improvements_Action_Plan.md:38-40](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_UI_Improvements_Action_Plan.md#L38-L40)

## Typography

The typography system differentiates between administrative/UI elements and narrative/marketing content.

*   **JetBrains Mono**: Used for UI labels, Billy AI surfaces, and technical data.
*   **Inter**: Reserved for consulting pages, marketing content, and formal documentation.
*   **Cabin Sketch**: Dedicated to specific brand wordmarks and the "Musical DNA" module.
*   **Man Rope / Script**: Used for the primary tagline and inspirational phrases.

Sources: [GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md), [spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:52-54](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L52-L54), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:246-250](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L246-L250), [GestaltView_UI_Improvements_Action_Plan.md:58-60](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_UI_Improvements_Action_Plan.md#L58-L60)

## Room-Specific Aesthetics

Each "room" in the GestaltView ecosystem has a unique aurora token and personality, governed by a specific Digital Intelligence (DI) persona.

```mermaid
graph TD
    subgraph Rooms
        A[Blackboard Room] -- Cyan Aurora --> B(Billy)
        C[Sanctuary] -- Emerald Aurora --> D(The Keeper)
        E[Creation Corner] -- Indigo Aurora --> F(The Art Teacher)
        G[Dynamic Inner World] -- Amber Aurora --> H(The Curator)
        I[External Scaffold] -- Cyan Aurora --> J(The Analyst)
        K[Musical DNA] -- Rose Aurora --> L(The Legend)
    end
    
    B -- "Concierge" --> M[Open Capture]
    D -- "Gentle" --> N[Private Reflection]
    F -- "Eccentric" --> O[Artifact Synthesis]
    H -- "Celebratory" --> P[Museum Exhibition]
```
The diagram above shows the relationship between specific Rooms, their Aurora color accents, and the corresponding AI personas.
Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:214-243](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L214-L243)

### Room Design Requirements
*   **Blackboard Room**: Atmospheric, cyan-toned, prioritizing "unencumbered capture." It must lack metaphysical prompts to avoid interrupting flow state.
*   **Creation Corner**: Purple/Indigo accents. It must feel like a "forge" where ideas become tangible artifacts.
*   **Sanctuary**: Warm, emerald-toned, low-stimulation space. It uses "notebooky" textures for the journal and higher transparency glass cards.

Sources: [spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:31-36](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L31-L36), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:267-300](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L267-L300), [GestaltView_UI_Improvements_Action_Plan.md:113-116](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_UI_Improvements_Action_Plan.md#L113-L116)

## Brand Voice and Persona

The brand voice is non-judgmental, inquisitive, and avoids "guru-speak" or marketing fluff.

### Persona Archetypes
| Name | Role/Voice | Key Quirk |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Billy** | Concierge / Bureaucrat | References obscure administrative procedures |
| **The Keeper** | Sanctuary Guardian | Speaks in short, quiet sentences; anti-guru |
| **The Art Teacher** | Creative Catalyst | Catastrophizes creatively; uses ALL CAPS for excitement |
| **The Curator** | Museum Guide | Remembers specific context users might forget |
| **The Legend** | Musical Archaeologist | Darkly comedic about survival; references B-sides |

Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:214-243](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L214-L243)

### Linguistic Constraints (The "Kill List")
To maintain the brand voice, certain "developer-facing" or "metaphysical" language is strictly forbidden in the UI:
*   **Forbidden**: "runtime", "forward wall", "orb approval rack", "low stimulation room."
*   **Encouraged**: Plain language that focuses on the experience, not the architecture. The system "holds" rather than "analyzes."

Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:112-140](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L112-L140)

## Technical Implementation of the Design System

### State-Driven Layouts
The `AdaptiveLayoutSystem` component adjusts the UI based on the user's cognitive state (Focused, Scattered, Creative, Low Energy). This includes theme switching, density adjustments, and motion toggles.

### Billy's Heartbeat (The AuroraOrb)
Billy's presence is represented by a floating orb that communicates status through size and glow.

| State | Motion/Behavior | Glow Radius |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Idle** | Slow 3s breathing | 30px |
| **Listening** | Fast 1s pulsing | 50px |
| **Speaking** | Ripple outward | 45px |
| **Thinking** | Slow spin | 35px |

Sources: [spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md:28-30](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/spec-1-uiux-refactor-gestaltview.md#L28-L30), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:198-208](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L198-L208)

## Conclusion

The UI Design System & Brand Voice of GestaltView v2.0 is a deliberate rejection of standard dashboard design. By combining the Neural Aurora visual language with a highly specific, persona-driven brand voice, the system creates a safe, accumulative container for human consciousness. Its technical rigor—from specific glow radii to the enforcement of the "Kill List"—ensures that the architecture remains invisible, leaving only the experience of self-discovery and creation.

Sources: [Technical_Spec.md:105-115](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Technical_Spec.md#L105-L115), [WhatIsGestaltView.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/WhatIsGestaltView.md), [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:144-150](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L144-L150)

# Authentication & Session Handling

Authentication and Session Handling in GestaltView is designed to transition from a complex, multi-user identity orchestration model toward a streamlined, single-user administrative flow. The primary objective is to provide a reliable login gate for the platform operator while eliminating latency and failure modes associated with remote authentication dependencies.

The system utilizes a combination of Supabase for database-level Row Level Security (RLS) and a bespoke administrative login flow for high-level operations. This hybrid approach ensures that while the broader infrastructure supports future multi-user orchestration, the current operating reality remains focused on immediate, low-latency access for the primary user.

Sources: [gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md:3-10](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md#L3-L10), [GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md:20-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md#L20-L25)

## 1. Authentication Architecture

The architecture is shifting toward a local-first authentication model to resolve issues with 401 response noise and authentication lookup timeouts. This transition involves replacing brittle session paths with an explicit, password-protected administrative gate.

### 1.1 Transition Strategy
The project is moving away from external magic-link or OAuth providers in favor of a signed cookie-based session model.

```mermaid
graph TD
    User[Operator/User] -->|Input Password| LoginUI[Login Page]
    LoginUI -->|POST /api/login| API[Auth API]
    API -->|Verify Hash| ENV[ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH]
    API -->|Issue Cookie| User
    User -->|Signed Cookie| Middleware[Auth Middleware]
    Middleware -->|Authorize| Routes[Protected Routes]
    Middleware -->|Reject 401| LoginUI
```
The diagram shows the transition to a local administrative flow where the server verifies a password hash stored in environment variables and issues a secure cookie.

Sources: [gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md:14-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md#L14-L25), [GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md:45-50](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md#L45-L50)

### 1.2 Data Layer Security (Supabase)
GestaltView leverages Supabase for data persistence and row-level security. All user-facing tables, such as `journals`, `scrapbook_items`, and `blueprints`, are scoped using RLS policies.

| Component | Logic / Constraint |
|-----------|--------------------|
| RLS Scoping | `FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id)` |
| Tables Protected | `journals`, `scrapbook_items`, `blueprints`, `insights`, `user_preferences` |
| Primary Key | `user_id` (UUID) |

Sources: [GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md:370-375](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Unified_Field_Spec_v1.0.md#L370-L375), [repo_manifest.md:15-20](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/repo_manifest.md#L15-L20)

## 2. Session Management

Session handling relies on secure, HTTP-only cookies to maintain state across the application. This ensures that the frontend does not need to repeatedly query remote auth providers for basic identity resolution.

### 2.1 Admin Session Model
The proposed administrative session model is lightweight and explicit:

```json
{
  "userId": "keith",
  "role": "admin",
  "issuedAt": 1716000000,
  "expiresAt": 1716086400
}
```
Sources: [gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md:42-49](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md#L42-L49)

### 2.2 Cookie Configuration
The session cookie is configured with strict security parameters to prevent client-side tampering or unauthorized access.

| Option | Value | Purpose |
|--------|-------|---------|
| `HttpOnly` | `true` | Prevents JavaScript access to the session token. |
| `Secure` | `true` | Ensures cookies are only sent over HTTPS. |
| `SameSite` | `Lax` or `Strict` | Protects against Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF). |
| `Signing` | `SESSION_SECRET` | Verifies cookie integrity on the server. |

Sources: [gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md:38-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md#L38-L45), [GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md:65-70](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md#L65-L70)

## 3. Implementation and Security Hardening

The system distinguishes between public-facing marketing pages and protected administrative surfaces. Access is controlled via a central middleware layer.

### 3.1 Protected Route Registry
Certain routes are designated as "Protected" and require a valid administrative session.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant U as User
    participant M as Middleware
    participant A as Protected API
    U->>M: Request /api/session/state
    alt Valid Cookie
        M->>A: Forward Request
        A-->>U: 200 OK (Data)
    else Invalid/Expired
        M-->>U: 401 Unauthorized / Redirect to /login
    end
```
The sequence diagram illustrates the middleware gate checking the signed cookie before allowing access to internal API routes.

Sources: [gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md:58-65](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md#L58-L65), [repo_manifest.md:40-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/repo_manifest.md#L40-L45)

### 3.2 Security Requirements
To prevent credential leaks, the platform enforces strict environment variable separation.

- **ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH**: Stored only as a hash; never in plaintext.
- **SESSION_SECRET**: Used to sign cookies; must remain server-side.
- **VITE_ Prefixes**: Strictly limited to public configuration. Sensitive LLM keys or auth secrets are prohibited from using this prefix to avoid inlining into the browser bundle.

Sources: [GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md:55-65](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md#L55-L65), [gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md:51-56](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md#L51-L56)

## 4. Operational Controls

For administrative and development purposes, a "Developer Override" mode exists. This allows for direct manipulation of user profiles and bypasses standard procedures when active.

| Command | Action |
|---------|--------|
| `Developer Override: ON` | Activates override mode; AI suspends standard SOPs. |
| `Developer Override: OFF` | Deactivates override mode; returns to normal operations. |
| `Go to Module [Name]` | Bypasses standard navigation for direct access. |
| `Show Log` | Displays an audit trail of all override actions. |

Sources: [Developer OverRide User Seed Prompt Version 1.5.md:200-215](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Developer%20OverRide%20User%20Seed%20Prompt%20Version%201.5.md#L200-L215)

## 5. Conclusion

The Authentication & Session Handling system in GestaltView prioritizes operational stability for the primary operator. By transitioning to a local-first, signed cookie model, the project eliminates external dependencies that caused latency and errors. This structure provides a secure foundation for current use cases while maintaining the necessary data scoping (via Supabase RLS) to support future multi-user capabilities.

Sources: [gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md:130-135](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/gestaltview-auth-transition-spec.md#L130-L135), [COLAB.md:40-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/COLAB.md#L40-L45)

# AI Routing & Fallback Mechanism

The AI Routing & Fallback Mechanism in GestaltView is a critical architectural layer designed to ensure high availability and cost-effective utilization of Large Language Models (LLMs). It acts as a gateway that orchestrates requests between various AI providers, primarily prioritizing **Gemini Flash 2.0** for core conversational experiences while maintaining a robust "free-first" cascade to fallback providers such as OpenAI and Anthropic.

This system is central to the operation of **Billy**, the platform's primary AI companion, and various specialized Digital Intelligences (DIs) found within specialized modules like the [Resume Lab](#resume-lab) and [SymbioCoder](#symbiocoder). By abstracting the specific provider logic behind a unified `llmRouter`, the platform protects itself from model churn and vendor-specific outages.

Sources: [AGENTS.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/AGENTS.md), [GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md), [audits/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md:27-35](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/audits/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md#L27-L35)

## AI Provider Hierarchy

The routing mechanism follows a strict priority list to manage performance and reliability. Gemini Flash 2.0 is the mandated primary engine for core Billy functions. If the primary provider fails or is unreachable, the system cascades through established fallback tiers.

| Priority | Provider | Implementation Note |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Primary** | Google Gemini Flash 2.0 | Mandatory for BillyLive.tsx and Billy.tsx |
| **Secondary (Fallback)** | OpenAI | Used when Gemini is unavailable or for specific task routing |
| **Tertiary (Fallback)** | Anthropic | Final cloud-based fallback tier |
| **Experimental/Local** | Groq / Ollama | Supported for high-speed or local-only AI processing |

Sources: [AGENTS.md:16-18](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/AGENTS.md#L16-L18), [GEMINI.md:12-14](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md#L12-L14), [audits/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md:27-35](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/audits/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md#L27-L35)

## Architecture and Data Flow

The AI routing logic is centralized within the `api/_lib/llmRouter.ts` (backend) and `client/src/lib/llmrouter.ts` (frontend) modules. This abstraction ensures that individual room components do not need to manage API keys or provider-specific request formats directly.

### Request Routing Logic
When a component or module requires an AI completion, it sends a request to the `llmRouter`. The router evaluates the request based on parameters such as `preferFree` or specific model requirements.

```mermaid
graph TD
    UserRequest[User Request] --> Router[llmRouter Gateway]
    Router --> CheckPrimary{Primary Available?}
    CheckPrimary -- Yes --> Gemini[Gemini Flash 2.0]
    CheckPrimary -- No --> Fallback1[OpenAI Fallback]
    Fallback1 --> Success1{Success?}
    Success1 -- No --> Fallback2[Anthropic Fallback]
    Gemini --> Return[Return AI Response]
    Fallback1 -- Yes --> Return
    Fallback2 --> Return
```
The diagram shows the cascade logic used to ensure a response is generated even if the primary Google AI Studio endpoint is down.
Sources: [AGENTS.md:175-177](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/AGENTS.md#L175-L177), [specs/root/workspaces/spec-workspace-module-integration.md:6-10](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/workspaces/spec-workspace-module-integration.md#L6-L10), [audits/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md:167-175](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/audits/GestaltView_Repository_Deep_Dive_Audit_Report.md#L167-L175)

## Integration with Specialized Modules

Specialized workspaces and modules utilize the `llmRouter` to maintain identity consistency and handle complex task orchestration. For example, the `SymbioCoder` and `Insight Bot` modules route all LLM calls through the existing cascade to ensure "free-first" processing while leveraging specific prompt templates.

### Workspace AI Proxies
Server-side proxies forward authenticated requests to microservice backends (like FastAPI for Resume Rockstar) while keeping AI credentials hidden from the client.

| Endpoint | Sub-Room | Routing Behavior |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `/api/workspace/resume/enhance` | Resume Lab | Routes via `llmRouter` with `preferFree: true` |
| `/api/workspace/insight/chat` | Insight Analysis | Free-first cascade; supports Web/Reddit/Discord context |
| `/api/workspace/code/generate` | SymbioCoder | Routes through `llmRouter`; passes `cognitive_state` |

Sources: [specs/root/workspaces/spec-workspace-module-integration.md:38-70](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/root/workspaces/spec-workspace-module-integration.md#L38-L70)

### Generative Engine Integration
The Generative Engine uses the router to perform "Intentional Synthesis" and "Ambient Coherence" scans. This includes routing for:
*   **PLK Resonance Passes:** Scoring text against the User's Personal Language Key.
*   **Artifact Synthesis:** Generating Markdown, PDF-ready HTML, or code snippets from captured "orbs".

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant UI as Creation Corner
    participant API as Gen-Engine API
    participant Router as LLM Router
    participant LLM as AI Provider (Gemini/OpenAI)

    UI->>API: POST /api/gen-engine/artifacts
    API->>Router: route(messages, style_context)
    Router->>LLM: Request completion
    LLM-->>Router: Response data
    Router-->>API: Unified response object
    API-->>UI: Generated Artifact + Provenance
```
This sequence demonstrates how the Generative Engine abstracts complex LLM calls through the unified routing layer to produce artifacts with provenance.
Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:310-335](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L310-L335)

## Implementation Constraints

The mechanism is subject to several mandatory developer rules to maintain system integrity and "Neural Aurora" branding:
1.  **Full File Replacement:** AI agents must always return complete files when modifying routing logic to prevent merge conflicts.
2.  **Gemini Mandate:** The primary companion (Billy) MUST use Gemini Flash 2.0. Switch-overs to OpenAI or Anthropic for core Billy functions are considered bugs unless the primary service is objectively down.
3.  **No Snippet Edits:** Surgical edits to the `llmRouter` are prohibited to ensure that the complex fallback logic remains cohesive.

Sources: [AGENTS.md:35-43](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/AGENTS.md#L35-L43), [GEMINI.md:33-40](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md#L33-L40)

## Summary
The AI Routing & Fallback Mechanism provides the "Brain" of GestaltView with its necessary resilience and flexibility. By centralizing LLM access through a prioritized cascade (Gemini → OpenAI → Anthropic), the system maintains its commitment to being a reliable "consciousness-serving" platform that remains operational regardless of individual provider status or cost constraints.

### Voice Processing Services
