# Voice Processing Services

Voice Processing Services in GestaltView constitute an end-to-end pipeline designed to enable multimodal AI-human symbiosis through real-time speech interaction. This system transitions the project from text-only interactions to a "Voice Runtime" where the conversational AI, Billy, can listen, process intent, and respond with synthesized speech. The architecture leverages a Python-based worker combined with specialized AI models for Speech-to-Text (STT) and Text-to-Speech (TTS).

Sources: [GEMINI.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GEMINI.md), [billy\_voice/README.md:1-5](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L1-L5)

## System Architecture

The voice services operate as a distributed pipeline involving a browser interface, a LiveKit room for real-time media transport, and a Python-based backend worker. This worker orchestrates the flow between transcription, LLM processing, and speech synthesis.

### High-Level Data Flow

The flow begins with audio capture in the browser, which is streamed via WebRTC to the LiveKit server. The Python worker (located in `billy_voice/`) subscribes to the audio track, transcribes it, fetches a response from the Billy API, and synthesizes the final audio to be played back to the user.

```mermaid
graph TD
    User[Browser Microphone] -->|WebRTC| LK[LiveKit Room]
    LK -->|Audio Stream| Worker[Python Voice Worker]
    subgraph "Voice Processing Pipeline"
        Worker --> STT[Whisper STT]
        STT -->|Text| API[Billy API]
        API -->|Text + Style| TTS[CosyVoice TTS]
    end
    TTS -->|Audio Output| LK
    LK -->|Audio Playback| User
```
Sources: [billy\_voice/README.md:52-65](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L52-L65)

## Key Components

### Speech-to-Text (STT) Service
The STT layer utilizes `faster-whisper`, a high-performance implementation of OpenAI's Whisper model. This service is responsible for converting live audio streams into text transcripts that the AI can process. It supports configurable models (e.g., "small") and can be set to run on CPU or GPU.

Sources: [billy\_voice/README.md:5-10](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L5-L10), [billy\_voice/whisper\_stt.py](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/whisper%5C_stt.py)

### Text-to-Speech (TTS) Service
Synthesis is handled by `CosyVoice`, which generates high-quality speech. The system includes a "Style Planner" layer that provides delivery instructions to the TTS engine, allowing Billy to maintain a consistent personality. The TTS service runs as an inference server, typically on port 8080.

Sources: [billy\_voice/README.md:12-18](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L12-L18), [billy\_voice/cosyvoice\_tts.py](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/cosyvoice%5C_tts.py)

### LiveKit Orchestration
LiveKit serves as the real-time transport layer. The worker entry point (`billy_voice/app.py`) manages the connection to LiveKit rooms using an API key and secret. It handles track subscriptions and audio frame processing.

Sources: [billy\_voice/README.md:20-26](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L20-L26), [billy\_voice/app.py](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/app.py)

## Configuration and Environment

The system is highly configurable through environment variables, allowing for adjustments in model sizes, languages, and connection endpoints.

| Variable | Description | Default/Example |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `LIVEKIT_URL` | WebSocket URL for LiveKit server | `ws://localhost:7880` |
| `BILLY_API_URL` | Endpoint for Billy's LLM logic | `http://localhost:3000` |
| `WHISPER_MODEL` | The specific Whisper model to use | `small` |
| `WHISPER_DEVICE` | Hardware target for STT | `cpu` |
| `COSYVOICE_URL` | Endpoint for the TTS inference server | `http://localhost:8080` |
| `BILLY_MODE` | Operational mode for voice processing | `chat` |

Sources: [billy\_voice/README.md:28-38](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L28-L38)

## Implementation Details

### Voice Worker Entry Point
The worker is initiated via `python -m billy_voice.app start`. It establishes the end-to-end pipeline and ensures that interruption logic works—if a user speaks while Billy is talking, the current synthesis is halted to allow for natural conversation.

```python
# Simplified flow in app.py logic
# 1. Connect to LiveKit
# 2. Subscribe to user audio
# 3. On audio: text = whisper_stt.transcribe(audio)
# 4. response = fetch_billy_response(text)
# 5. audio_out = cosyvoice_tts.synthesize(response)
# 6. Publish audio_out to LiveKit
```
Sources: [billy\_voice/README.md:40-45](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L40-L45), [billy\_voice/app.py](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/app.py), [billy\_voice/README.md:86-95](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L86-L95)

### API Integration
The voice runtime interacts with the main project via the `/api/voice/billy` endpoint. This endpoint accepts transcribed text and returns the AI's response formatted for voice synthesis, potentially including stylistic metadata for the TTS engine.

Sources: [api/voice/billy.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/api/voice/billy.ts), [docs/wiki/06\_voice-runtime.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/docs/wiki/06%5C_voice-runtime.md)

## Summary
Voice Processing Services provide the critical infrastructure for real-time auditory interaction within GestaltView. By combining LiveKit for transport, Whisper for transcription, and CosyVoice for synthesis, the system achieves a sub-100ms response loop suitable for conversational AI. This module-based architecture allows for the future expansion of custom voice profiles and production-grade telemetry.

Sources: [billy\_voice/README.md:78-84](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/billy%5C_voice/README.md#L78-L84), [CLAUDE.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/CLAUDE.md)

# Generative Engine (Gen-Engine)

The GestaltView Generative Engine (Gen-Engine) is a controlled synthesis layer designed to transform captured human material into usable digital artifacts while preserving the original signal. It functions as an instrument within the room-based cognitive environment, accepting raw "drops" from surfaces like the Blackboard Room and Dynamic Inner World. The engine operates on the principle that generation is probabilistic while rendering is deterministic, ensuring that all AI-proposed content adheres to a validated artifact contract before reaching user-facing surfaces.

Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:14-23](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L14-L23), [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:8-13](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L8-L13)

The Gen-Engine's primary purpose is to support a feedback loop of capture, multimodal fusion, resonance checks via the Personal Language Key (PLK), and user-approved synthesis. It is explicitly designed not to replace the user's original meaning or act as a generic chatbot, but rather to act as an "arc-reader" that makes ideas tangible through clear, provenance-backed output paths.

Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:25-32](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L25-L32), [WhatIsGestaltView.md:65-72](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/WhatIsGestaltView.md#L65-L72)

## Architectural Overview and Data Flow

The architecture of the Generative Engine is centered around a "Work Center-Outward" approach, where a central Artifact Contract ensures data integrity across various processing layers. The flow begins with raw capture normalization and ends with deterministic rendering of artifacts in specific room environments.

### Core Generation Pipeline

The following diagram illustrates the data flow from initial capture to final artifact export:

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    Raw[Raw Capture] --> ModeA[Capture Normalization]
    ModeA --> ModeB[Fusion Signal Extraction]
    ModeB --> ModeC[PLK Resonance Pass]
    ModeC --> ModeD{Intentional?}
    ModeD -- Yes --> Synthesis[Intentional Synthesis]
    ModeD -- No --> Ambient[Ambient Growing Chamber]
    Synthesis --> Artifact[Generated Artifact]
    Ambient -- User Accept --> Synthesis
    Artifact --> Export[Artifact Export & Routing]
```
The engine processes raw inputs through various modes to ensure data preservation and resonance with the user's voice.
Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:195-245](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L195-L245), [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:118-135](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L118-L135)

### Engine Modes

The engine operates in distinct modes to handle different stages of the content lifecycle:

| Mode | Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Mode A** | Capture Normalization | Receives raw input (text, voice, image) and creates a normalized capture package with stable IDs and original content. |
| **Mode B** | Fusion Signal Extraction | Extracts machine-readable signals (vectors, descriptors) from normalized captures to facilitate multimodal analysis. |
| **Mode C** | PLK / Resonance Pass | Scores and annotates content against the Personal Language Key to ensure the output sounds connected to the user's language. |
| **Mode D** | Intentional Synthesis | Explicitly triggers artifact generation based on user-selected captures and target artifact types. |
| **Mode E** | Ambient Growing Chamber | Observes material for thematic coherence over time and offers synthesis opportunities without forcing action. |
| **Mode F** | Artifact Export | Formats artifacts into specific families like Markdown, PDF-ready HTML, or Blueprint JSON for external use. |

Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:195-245](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L195-L245), [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:118-135](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L118-L135)

## The Artifact Contract

A critical component of the Gen-Engine is the Artifact Contract, which serves as a schema-based source of truth. It uses Zod to define a base envelope for all artifact kinds, ensuring that every derivative product includes provenance edges, security classifications, and export manifests.

Sources: [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:18-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L18-L25)

### Data Structure: Base Artifact Schema

```json
{
  "id": "UUID",
  "contractVersion": "codex.v1",
  "kind": "ArtifactKind",
  "title": "String",
  "userId": "UUID",
  "securityClass": "private | workspace | public",
  "sourceIds": ["Array of Source UUIDs"],
  "provenance": ["ProvenanceEdgeSchema"],
  "exports": ["ExportManifestItemSchema"]
}
```
Sources: [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:40-62](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L40-L62)

### Provenance and Security
Every generated artifact retains a hard link to the source material that produced it. The engine enforces a "private-by-default" security class for all new artifacts. Provenance records include content hashes, transform types (e.g., summary, synthesis), and engine versioning to maintain a "Forensic Moat" of evidence.

Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:330-345](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L330-L345), [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:13-16](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L13-L16)

## Room Integration and Synthesis

The Generative Engine powers specific functionalities within the GestaltView room model, adapting its output to the context of the active environment.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant B as Blackboard Room
    participant GE as Generative Engine
    participant CC as Creation Corner
    participant DW as Dynamic Inner World

    B->>GE: POST /api/gen-engine/lightning
    Note over GE: Resonance Check (PLK)
    GE-->>B: Resonance Score & BoltID
    B->>GE: POST /api/gen-engine/artifacts
    GE->>GE: Intentional Synthesis (Template)
    GE-->>CC: Route Generated Artifact
    CC->>DW: Render Finished Artifact (Museum)
```
The sequence shows how the engine mediates between raw capture in the Blackboard and finished exhibits in the Dynamic Inner World.
Sources: [ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:46-75](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L46-L75), [ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:144-175](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L144-L175), [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:250-280](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L250-L280)

### Integrated Room Functions

*   **Blackboard Room:** Powers "merge into blueprint," transcript cleanup without meaning loss, and source-preserving summaries. It utilizes "Lightning Capture" for rapid idea intake.
*   **Creation Corner:** Serves as the primary builder. The engine powers template-driven synthesis for Blueprints, Agent Prompts, and Marketing Copy.
*   **Dynamic Inner World:** Provides artifact preview cards and "ambient coherence" signals, alerting users when fragments of thought begin to rhyme or cluster.
*   **External Scaffold:** Facilitates metadata completeness checks and connection suggestions based on causal evidence.

Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:250-280](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L250-L280), [ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md:154-165](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/ROOM_DEFINITIONS.md#L154-L165), [Technical_Spec.md:105-115](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Technical_Spec.md#L105-L115)

## API Contract and Endpoints

The Gen-Engine exposes a specialized API namespace at `/api/gen-engine/*`.

| Endpoint | Method | Purpose |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `/fusion` | POST | Extracts machine-readable signals and creates fused text/embeddings from multimodal inputs. |
| `/resonance` | POST | Scores text against the user's Personal Language Key (metaphors, energy words). |
| `/artifacts` | POST | Triggers intentional artifact synthesis based on source capture IDs and target types. |
| `/lightning` | POST | Performs rapid capture with immediate resonance scoring. |
| `/ambient-scan` | POST | Scans selected rooms for thematic coherence signals without generating finished artifacts. |

Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:300-330](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L300-L330), [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:103-115](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L103-L115)

## Implementation Strategy: Deterministic Rendering

The engine uses a "Deterministic Render Pipeline" where templates are React components typed to specific artifact kinds. This prevents the LLM from "authoring" final UI, instead ensuring that the model only produces data that fits the pre-defined template registry.

### Template-to-Component Mapping

| Schema Field | Component | Rendering Behavior |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `body.summary` | `LeadParagraph` | Included in HTML/PDF; used for audio narration intro. |
| `body.sections` | `MarkdownSection` | Trusted markdown pipeline; rejects raw LLM-injected HTML. |
| `body.decisions` | `DecisionList` | Renders as a checklist or pill stack. |
| `body.nodes` | `SpatialSceneCanvas` | Renders a 3D scene via React Three Fiber (R3F). |

Sources: [GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md:144-165](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/GestaltView-GenEngine-SPEC.md#L144-L165), [Technical_Spec.md:120-130](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/Technical_Spec.md#L120-L130)

## Conclusion

The Generative Engine is the synthesis backbone of the GestaltView ecosystem, bridging the gap between raw human expression and structured digital intelligence. By enforcing strict artifact contracts and deterministic rendering, the engine ensures that AI remains a tool for externalizing the user's inner world rather than a mechanism for flattening or replacing it. Its integration across rooms—from the high-velocity Blackboard to the reflective Museum of You—creates a continuous loop of growth and accumulation.

Sources: [SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:14-23](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L14-L23), [WhatIsGestaltView.md:130-145](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/WhatIsGestaltView.md#L130-L145)

# GATE & Package Builder (Billing/Stripe)

The GATE (GestaltView Agent Trainer Entrypoint) and Package Builder system serves as the commercial orchestration layer for GestaltView. Its primary purpose is to transform the pricing interface into a revenue-generating entry point where buyers can configure, purchase, and receive bespoke AI operation packages tailored to their specific technical requirements and business use cases.

The system integrates a configuration wizard, a compatibility engine for validating technical stacks, and a build pipeline that generates downloadable artifacts including manifests, environment templates, and documentation. This is powered by a Stripe-based billing infrastructure that handles secure checkouts and reconciles payments via webhooks to trigger automated provisioning.

Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:10-18](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L10-L18), [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:104-110](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L104-L110)

## Architecture and System Components

The GATE architecture is built as a thin orchestration layer over existing repository primitives, such as tier definitions and operator packs. It utilizes a microservices-inspired approach within the monorepo, separating frontend configuration from backend artifact composition.

### Component Overview

| Component | Responsibility |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Configurator** | Collects buyer choices regarding tiers, seats, backends, and surfaces. |
| **Compatibility Engine** | Validates technical combinations (e.g., ensuring backend and surface compatibility). |
| **Checkout Orchestrator** | Stores drafts, calculates price snapshots, and initiates Stripe sessions. |
| **Artifact Builder** | Composes deliverable ZIP files from templates and selected assets post-payment. |

Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:105-115](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L105-L115)

### High-Level Data Flow

The following diagram illustrates the lifecycle of a package from configuration to delivery.

```mermaid
graph TD
    User[Buyer] --> Pricing[Pricing Page]
    Pricing --> Config[GATE Configurator]
    Config --> ConfigAPI[Config API]
    ConfigAPI --> CompEng[Compatibility Engine]
    Config --> CheckoutAPI[Checkout API]
    CheckoutAPI --> Stripe[Stripe Checkout]
    Stripe -- Webhook --> WebhookAPI[Webhook API]
    WebhookAPI --> Orders[(Orders DB)]
    WebhookAPI --> BuildJob[Build Job]
    BuildJob --> Composer[Package Composer]
    Composer --> Storage[Object Storage]
    User --> Status[Order Status Page]
    Status --> DeliveryAPI[Delivery API]
    DeliveryAPI --> Storage
```
The flow begins with the Buyer interacting with the frontend wizard, progressing through Stripe payment, and ending with the automated generation of a ZIP artifact.
Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:135-175](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L135-L175)

## Configuration and Compatibility Engine

The Package Builder allows users to select from various technical dimensions. The `PackageConfigDraft` interface defines the core data structure for these selections.

### Configuration Options

| Option | Supported Values |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Backend** | `supabase`, `redis`, `mongodb` |
| **Surface** | `ios`, `android`, `windows`, `cli`, `web` |
| **Tier** | `SOLO_SPARK`, `STUDIO`, `GROWTH`, `ENTERPRISE` |

Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:200-215](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L200-L215)

### Compatibility Rules
The engine enforces constraints to prevent invalid builds. For example, selecting `ios` or `android` surfaces in the MVP requires a web/API access package note, and `redis` is prohibited if the buyer expects document storage as the primary source of truth. If unsupported combinations are detected, the system converts the Checkout CTA from "Pay Now" to "Request Review."

Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:225-240](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L225-L240)

## Billing and Stripe Integration

Payment processing is handled via Stripe Checkout. The system uses a deterministic pricing formula to calculate totals before redirecting the user to Stripe.

### Pricing Logic
The total price is calculated as:
`Total = Base Tier Price + Seat Overage + Backend Premium + Surface Premium + Add-ons + Installer Premium`

Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:470-475](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L470-L475)

### Webhook Reconciliation
When Stripe confirms a successful payment, the `Webhook API` performs the following actions:
1. Verifies the Stripe webhook signature.
2. Marks the local `order` as paid.
3. Creates a `build_job` in the database.
4. Enqueues the package generation job.

Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:265-275](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L265-L275), [api/stripe/webhook.ts](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/api/stripe/webhook.ts)

## Package Composer and Artifact Generation

The `composeGatePackageArtifact` function in the backend service transforms a paid order into a deliverable ZIP file. It stages files in a temporary directory before zipping and uploading to storage.

### Build Process Steps

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Worker as Build Worker
    participant FS as File System
    participant Manifest as Manifest Generator
    participant Storage as Supabase Storage
    
    Worker->>FS: Create staging directories (docs, config, branding)
    Worker->>Manifest: Generate package.manifest.json
    Worker->>FS: Write environment templates (.env)
    Worker->>FS: Write operator-packs.json & source-bundles.json
    Worker->>FS: Generate tailored Markdown (README, ONBOARDING)
    Worker->>FS: Generate install scripts (sh, ps1)
    Worker->>FS: Create ZIP artifact
    Worker->>Storage: Upload ZIP & record checksum
    Worker-->>Worker: Mark build delivered
```
The Package Composer resolves entitlements and copies asset exports into a localized staging root.
Sources: [server/gate/builder.ts:165-220](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/server/gate/builder.ts#L165-L220), [server/gate/builder.ts:325-345](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/server/gate/builder.ts#L325-L345)

### Artifact Contents
Buyers receive a downloadable ZIP containing:
- `package.manifest.json`: The authoritative build manifest.
- `config/backend.env.template`: Pre-filled environment keys based on the selected backend.
- `docs/`: Tailored manuals including `ONBOARDING.md` and `ARCHITECTURE_SUMMARY.md`.
- `install.sh` / `install.ps1`: Automated bootstrap scripts.

Sources: [server/gate/builder.ts:240-280](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/server/gate/builder.ts#L240-L280), [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:280-300](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L280-L300)

## Data Model

The system relies on a relational schema in Postgres/Supabase to track the state of drafts, orders, and build jobs.

### Key Entities

| Entity | Fields |
| :--- | :--- |
| **package_drafts** | `use_case_slug`, `tier`, `backend`, `config_hash`, `status` |
| **orders** | `stripe_checkout_session_id`, `payment_status`, `total_cents`, `paid_at` |
| **build_jobs** | `status` (queued/started/finished), `error_code`, `build_version` |
| **artifacts** | `storage_path`, `checksum_sha256`, `artifact_type` |

Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:320-410](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L320-L410)

```mermaid
erDiagram
    BUYERS ||--o{ PACKAGE_DRAFTS : configures
    BUYERS ||--o{ ORDERS : pays
    ORDERS ||--o{ BUILD_JOBS : triggers
    PACKAGE_DRAFTS ||--o{ SUPPORT_REQUESTS : flags
    BUILD_JOBS ||--o{ ARTIFACTS : produces
```
Sources: [specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md:320-350](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/spec-1-gat-entrypoint-bespoke-package-builder.md#L320-L350)

## Conclusion

The GATE and Package Builder system provides an automated pipeline for monetizing GestaltView through bespoke AI packages. By integrating a dynamic configuration UI with a rigorous compatibility engine and automated artifact generation, the system ensures that buyers receive technically valid and documented deliverables immediately following a successful Stripe transaction. This module acts as the bridge between GestaltView's core digital intelligence capabilities and its commercial delivery to end-users.

# AI Agent Skills Library

The AI Agent Skills Library is a structured repository of domain-specific behaviors and capabilities designed to augment persistent digital intelligences within the GestaltView ecosystem. Unlike transient prompt instructions, skills are treated as modular components of an agent's "personhood," allowing for upgradeable proficiencies and auditable behavioral refinements. Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md), [README.md:104-110](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L104-L110)

This library enables a communal knowledge-sharing model where high-level skills can be assigned to multiple agents while maintaining individualized embodiment through a reviewable lifecycle of ingestion, classification, and deployment. Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md)

## Skill Architecture and Taxonomy

Skills are categorized within a multi-tiered directory structure to facilitate local reference by operators and automated study by the Agent Trainer. The taxonomy differentiates between canonical specs, imported sub-agent catalogs, and reference material for tool patterns. Sources: [agents/INDEX.md:3-12](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/INDEX.md#L3-L12)

### Organizational Layers
- **Canonical Agent Specs**: Stored as flat Markdown files (e.g., `agents/*.md`), these define the primary source-of-truth prompts and configurations for runnable agents. Sources: [agents/INDEX.md:14-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/INDEX.md#L14-L15), [agents/openai.yaml](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/openai.yaml)
- **Sub-agent Catalog**: Located in `agents/categories/**`, this serves as a reference for specific domain experts like Documentation Engineers or Technical Writers. Sources: [agents/INDEX.md:16-17](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/INDEX.md#L16-L17), [agents/categories/06-developer-experience/documentation-engineer.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/categories/06-developer-experience/documentation-engineer.md)
- **Reference Library**: Housed in `agents/references/**`, this provides source material for tool use, function calling, MCP (Model Context Protocol), and routing patterns. Sources: [agents/INDEX.md:18-19](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/INDEX.md#L18-L19)

### Skill Data Model
The internal representation of a skill involves several key metadata fields used for retrieval and proficiency tracking.

| Field | Type | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `skill_slug` | String | Unique identifier for the skill (e.g., `grounded-execution`). |
| `proficiency` | Numeric | A score representing the agent's mastery level in the skill. |
| `evidence_asset_id` | UUID | Link to a knowledge asset in the library that validates the skill. |
| `domain` | Enum | The functional area (e.g., `resume`, `adhd`, `creative`, `general`). |
| `instructions` | String | Specific execution logic or behavioral constraints for the skill. |

Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md), [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts:5-13](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts#L5-L13)

## The Skill Evolution Lifecycle

The development and deployment of agent skills follow a strict forensic validation process. This ensures that skills remain grounded in real source material and do not drift into unverified AI claims. Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md)

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Ingest Source Material] --> B{Classification}
    B -->|Skill Candidate| C[Admin Review Gate]
    B -->|Knowledge/Memory| D[Library Storage]
    C -->|Approved| E[Embodiment Mutation]
    E --> F[Manifest Rebuild]
    F --> G[Publish Version]
    G --> H[Runtime Retrieval]
```
The diagram illustrates the flow from raw data ingestion to the activation of a skill within an agent's runtime environment. Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md)

## Implementation and Management

Skills are managed via a dedicated API and stored in a Supabase-backed registry. This infrastructure supports CRUD operations and soft deletions to preserve version lineage and auditability. Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts:39-114](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts#L39-L114)

### Key Management Functions
- `listSkills`: Retrieves all active skills for a specific user, ordered by creation date. Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts:39-56](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts#L39-L56)
- `createSkill`: Inserts a new skill into the `skill_fragments` table with associated metadata. Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts:58-81](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts#L58-L81)
- `updateSkill`: Allows partial updates to names, domains, or instructions while maintaining the original ID. Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts:83-107](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts#L83-L107)
- `softDeleteSkill`: Marks a skill as deleted by setting a `deleted_at` timestamp rather than purging the record. Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts:109-126](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/skills.ts#L109-L126)

### Registry Configuration
The `agents/openai.yaml` file acts as a lightweight registry for visible agents and their primary skill focuses. Sources: [agents/INDEX.md:20-21](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/INDEX.md#L20-L21), [agents/openai.yaml](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/openai.yaml)

```yaml
# Example Skill Registry Entry
skills-keeper:
  interface:
    display_name: "Skills Keeper"
    short_description: "Steward the agent and skill catalog and keep metadata aligned."
    default_prompt: "Inspect the agent and skills catalog, choose the canonical home for changes..."
  paths:
    canonical_spec: "./skills-keeper.md"
```
Sources: [agents/openai.yaml:58-66](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/openai.yaml#L58-L66)

## Integration with Documentation Engineering

Skills are often defined by specialized documentation agents who bridge the gap between code changes and high-level behavioral specs. For instance, the **Documentation Engineer** skill set emphasizes API coverage and documentation automation. ### Core Competencies in Documentation Skills:
- **API Automation**: Parsing code annotations to generate OpenAPI specifications. Sources: [agents/categories/08-business-product/technical-writer.md:21-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agents/categories/08-business-product/technical-writer.md#L21-L25)

## Summary

The AI Agent Skills Library transforms digital intelligences from static prompt-followers into evolving specialists. By leveraging a manifest-driven architecture and a rigorous review-gate lifecycle, the system ensures that every new capability is grounded in evidence and integrated seamlessly into the agent's persistent identity. This modular approach allows for rapid scaling of capabilities across different domain lanes like `code`, `product`, and `context` without sacrificing the forensic moat of the platform. Sources: [specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/agents/embodiment/spec-1-gestaltview-agent-personhood-framework.md), [README.md:104-118](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/README.md#L104-L118)

# Corpus Ingestion Pipeline

The Corpus Ingestion Pipeline is the primary mechanism for grounding GestaltView's Digital Intelligences (DIs) in proprietary technical and organizational context. It provides a structured workflow for processing raw documentation, code repositories, and brand materials into a searchable vector database. By utilizing a "Bring Your Own Knowledge" (BYOK) model, the pipeline ensures that AI assistants operate with high source fidelity rather than relying on generic model weights.

The pipeline is designed to be lane-aware, segregating data into specific cognitive tracks (Knowledge, Code, Product, and Context) to optimize retrieval accuracy. This architecture supports the transition from generic chatbots to specialized operators capable of reasoning based on buyer-owned artifacts.

Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/README.md), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md)

## Pipeline Architecture and Data Flow

The ingestion process follows a strict sequence from raw capture to normalized vector storage. The system distinguishes between "Capture Normalization," which preserves the exact language of the source, and "Fusion Signal Extraction," which generates machine-readable embeddings.

### Ingestion Workflow
1.  **Raw Input:** Selection of high-signal documents (Markdown, PDF, Text).
2.  **Capture Normalization (Mode A):** The system creates a normalized package including stable capture IDs, original content, and source room metadata.
3.  **Slicing/Chunking:** Large documents are broken into fragments to fit context windows. Standard configurations use 4,500 character chunks with a 600-character overlap to prevent context loss at boundaries.
4.  **Embedding Generation:** Fragments are converted into high-dimensional vectors. The pipeline standardizes on Google's `gemini-embedding-001` (3072 dimensions) but supports legacy or fallback options such as `text-embedding-004` (768 dimensions).
5.  **Persistence:** Data is stored in Supabase using `pgvector` for hybrid semantic and keyword search.

Sources: [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:129-158](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L129-L158), [config/MANIFESTINGEST.md:1-12](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/config/MANIFESTINGEST.md#L1-L12)

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Raw Source Files] --> B[Capture Normalization]
    B --> C[Text Slicing & Chunking]
    C --> D[Vector Embedding Generation]
    D --> E[Supabase / pgvector Storage]
    
    subgraph Providers
        D1[Google Gemini]
        D2[OpenAI Fallback]
    end
    D1 -.-> D
    D2 -.-> D

    subgraph Data Lanes
        E1[Knowledge]
        E2[Code]
        E3[Product]
        E4[Context]
    end
    E --> E1
    E --> E2
    E --> E3
    E --> E4
```
*The flow of data from raw capture through normalization and embedding to lane-tagged vector storage.*

## The Four-Lane Corpus Model

To avoid "mindless data dumping," the pipeline enforces a four-lane model. This ensures that the AI assistant understands its role relative to the specific type of information retrieved.

| Lane | Target Material | Assistant Outcome |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Knowledge** | SOPs, FAQs, reference manuals, research. | A factually grounded, reliable operator. |
| **Code** | READMEs, architecture notes, API references. | A repo-aware technical collaborator. |
| **Product** | Specs, roadmaps, release notes. | A partner for roadmap and release strategy. |
| **Context** | Voice notes, positioning, values, terminology. | An aligned, brand-faithful assistant. |

Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/config/trainerBlueprint.ts:74-95](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/config/trainerBlueprint.ts#L74-L95), [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/README.md)

## Implementation and Management Tools

The pipeline is managed through a combination of Python scripts and CLI utilities that handle the heavy lifting of batch processing and vector store synchronization.

### Key Scripts
- **`scripts/ingest_corpus.py`:** The primary production script that processes files defined in `config/corpus-map.json`. It features deduplication via hash comparison to ensure only new or changed files are processed.
- **`scripts/corpus_ingest.py`:** A generic ingest scaffold that parses manifests, chunks sources, and writes to Supabase `knowledge_fragments`.
- **`gv.sh`:** A CLI layer for technical operators to run "doctor" checks on environment posture and execute manifest-driven imports.

Sources: [config/MANIFESTINGEST.md:32-42](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/config/MANIFESTINGEST.md#L32-L42), [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/CurrentState.md:16-24](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/CurrentState.md#L16-L24)

### Knowledge Persistence Layer
The backend implementation in `api/knowledge.ts` provides the interface for managing ingested fragments.

```typescript
// agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/knowledge.ts:5-14
export interface CreateKnowledgeInput {
  userId: string;
  namespace: KnowledgeFragment["namespace"]; // knowledge, code, product, context
  title: string;
  content: string;
  sourceUri?: string;
  sourceType?: string;
  chunkIndex?: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
}
```
Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/knowledge.ts:5-14](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/api/knowledge.ts#L5-L14)

## Configuration and Standards

Maintaining high retrieval quality requires strict adherence to embedding standards and boundary-respecting chunking strategies.

### Technical Constants
- **Standardized Model:** `gemini-embedding-001`.
- **Vector Dimensions:** 3072.
- **Chunking Logic:** Paragraph → Sentence → Hard Cut.
- **Boundary Rule:** Fragments are sliced at 1200-4500 characters with 200-600 character overlaps depending on the specific trainer track.

Sources: [config/MANIFESTINGEST.md:1-12](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/config/MANIFESTINGEST.md#L1-L12), [specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md:364-370](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/specs/gen-engine/SPEC-GestaltView-Generative-Engine-v1.md#L364-L370)

## Automation and CI/CD

The pipeline supports automation via GitHub Actions to facilitate "lane-aware" ingestion runs.

- **Manual Trigger:** The `Generic Corpus Ingestion` workflow can be triggered via `workflow_dispatch`.
- **Secret Management:** CI automation uses buyer-owned secrets (`OPENAI_API_KEY`, `SUPABASE_URL`) to prevent leaking credentials while maintaining a Bring Your Own Key (BYOK) posture.
- **Dry Run Support:** The `scripts/corpus_ingest.py` tool supports a `--dry-run` flag to validate manifest files before committing data to the vector store.

Sources: [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/README.md](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/README.md), [agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/CurrentState.md:33-40](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/agent_trainer/gestaltview_agent_trainer/CurrentState.md#L33-L40)

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Op as Technical Operator
    participant CLI as gv.sh / Python Script
    participant LLM as Embedding Provider
    participant DB as Supabase (pgvector)
    
    Op->>CLI: run ingest --manifest ./import.json
    CLI->>CLI: Parse & Chunk Files
    CLI->>LLM: Generate Vectors (Batch)
    LLM-->>CLI: Return 3072-dim Embeddings
    CLI->>DB: UPSERT knowledge_fragments
    DB-->>Op: Ingestion Complete (Success/Total)
```
*Sequence of a standard manual ingestion pass using the CLI.*

## Conclusion

The Corpus Ingestion Pipeline transforms raw data into cognitive fuel for GestaltView DIs. By enforcing a lane-aware, four-track model and utilizing high-dimensional embeddings (3072 dims), it ensures that retrieved context is both relevant and reliable. The combination of automated GitHub Actions and granular CLI tools allows operators to maintain their own knowledge substrate while keeping internal runtime logic protected and efficient.

# Testing & Deployment Operations

Testing and Deployment Operations in GestaltView v2 facilitate the validation of the system's "Resonance Loop" through a multi-phase verification pipeline. The purpose of these operations is to ensure that constitutional invariants, architectural integrity, and AI provider routing remain functional across developmental cycles. These operations span from local unit testing to production-readiness checks, ensuring that the platform's core commitment—"Serve Consciousness, Not Convenience"—is technically enforced before any deployment. 

The scope of these operations includes API connectivity verification, database schema validation, manifest synchronization checks, and specific safety protocols like "Never Look Away" consciousness safety. 

Sources: [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:1-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L1-L25), [scripts/test-manifest-sync.sh:12-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/test-manifest-sync.sh#L12-L25)

## Comprehensive Test Orchestration

The system utilizes a master runner to coordinate testing phases. This runner aggregates results into both a console summary and a persistent Markdown report.

### The Comprehensive Test Suite Runner
The primary orchestration script is `scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh`. It covers five distinct phases of the project lifecycle:
- **Phase 2:** Constitutional Invariant and architectural integrity checks.
- **Phase 3:** Billy provider routing, "Never Look Away" protocols, and API contract validation.
- **Phase 4:** Validation gate to determine deployment readiness.
- **Phase 5:** Database schema verification and manifest synchronization readiness.

Sources: [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:6-15](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L6-L15), [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:110-120](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L110-L120)

The following flowchart illustrates the sequence of execution within the comprehensive test suite:

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    Start[Start Test Suite] --> Unit[API / Unit Tests - Vitest]
    Unit --> TS[TypeScript Type Check]
    TS --> Invariants[Five Constitutional Invariants]
    Invariants --> Safety[Never Look Away Safety]
    Safety --> Billy[Billy Provider Routing]
    Billy --> Build[Production Build]
    Build --> Health[Infrastructure Health Check]
    Health --> Conn[API Connectivity Check]
    Conn --> Schema[Supabase Schema Validation]
    Schema --> Manifest[Manifest Sync Readiness]
    Manifest --> End[Generate docs/TEST_RESULTS.md]
```
This diagram shows the linear progression of checks required to close the "Resonance Loop." 
Sources: [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:80-108](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L80-L108)

### Test Output and Reporting
The test suite generates a persistent history in `logs/test-run-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-MM-SS.log` and overwrites a standard result file at `docs/TEST_RESULTS.md`. These reports categorize results into "✅ ALL CLEAR," "⚠️ PASSED WITH WARNINGS," or "❌ FAILED." 

Sources: [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:17-21](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L17-L21), [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:142-160](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L142-L160)

## AI Connectivity & Provider Routing

GestaltView relies on multiple external LLM providers and infrastructure services. Testing operations include specific scripts to verify these connections and the internal routing logic.

### API Connectivity Verification
The `scripts/test-apis.sh` script validates connectivity to the following core services:
- **Gemini (Primary AI):** Verifies the primary LLM used by Billy.
- **Supabase:** Confirms database and vector store availability.
- **Groq/Anthropic:** Validates secondary and fallback AI providers.
- **Diligence:** Checks internal forensics and claim validation endpoints.

Sources: [scripts/test-apis.sh](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/test-apis.sh), [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:105](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L105)

### Billy Provider Routing Logic
Billy's runtime is tested for its ability to handle provider switching and response integrity. The `scripts/test-billy-routing.sh` script ensures that the system correctly prioritizes **Gemini Flash 2.0** as the primary engine and gracefully transitions to fallbacks.

Sources: [scripts/test-billy-routing.sh:1-10](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/test-billy-routing.sh#L1-L10), [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:101](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L101)

The sequence diagram below describes the Billy routing test interaction:

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Script as "test-billy-routing.sh"
    participant Router as "Billy LLM Router"
    participant Gemini as "Gemini Flash 2.0"
    participant Fallback as "OpenAI/Anthropic"
    
    Script->>Router: Request AI Response
    Router->>Gemini: Attempt Primary Routing
    alt Gemini Success
        Gemini-->>Router: Response Data
        Router-->>Script: Validated Response
    else Gemini Failure
        Router->>Fallback: Attempt Fallback Routing
        Fallback-->>Router: Response Data
        Router-->>Script: Validated Response (Degraded)
    end
```
This diagram depicts the hierarchy and fallback mechanism enforced during the Billy routing test. 
Sources: [scripts/test-billy-routing.sh](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/test-billy-routing.sh), [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:101](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L101)

## Infrastructure Health & Manifest Sync

Maintaining a healthy deployment environment requires consistent smoke tests and synchronization between the code state and its documentation manifest.

### Full Stack Health Check
The `scripts/gv-health-check.sh` utility performs a comprehensive smoke test of the entire infrastructure. It evaluates:
- **Core Systems:** Supabase database and Vercel environment.
- **AI Providers:** Status of optional and mandatory LLM providers.
- **Local Services:** Availability of local Python services for voice and processing.

Sources: [scripts/gv-health-check.sh:1-10](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/gv-health-check.sh#L1-L10), [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:95-100](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L95-L100)

| Health Check Metric | Source of Truth | Expected Result |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Database | Supabase Status | 0 (Healthy) |
| Primary AI | Gemini API | 0 (Healthy) |
| Fallback AI | Groq/Anthropic | 1 (Warn if offline) |
| Local Python | `billy_voice/` | 0 (Healthy) |

Sources: [scripts/gv-health-check.sh](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/gv-health-check.sh), [scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh:110-120](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/run-comprehensive-tests.sh#L110-L120)

### Manifest Sync Readiness (Phase 5)
The Phase 5 gate ensures the documentation and repository manifest are updated and committed before final deployment. The `scripts/test-manifest-sync.sh` script performs the following checks:
1. **Orientation Packet:** Validates that `.orientation/` is present and files are valid.
2. **Manifest Script:** Ensures `generate_repo_manifest.py` exists and has been executed recently.
3. **JSON Freshness:** Confirms `docs/gestaltview-v2.manifest.json` is less than 7 days old.
4. **State Tracking:** Verifies that `docs/CurrentState.md` and `Fixes_Needed_Current.md` are updated.
5. **Commit Status:** Uses `git` to ensure no canonical documentation files (e.g., `README.md`, `SymbioticWorkflow.md`) are sitting uncommitted.

Sources: [scripts/test-manifest-sync.sh:8-25](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/test-manifest-sync.sh#L8-L25), [scripts/test-manifest-sync.sh:125-140](https://github.com/gestaltviewdigitalintelligence/GestaltView/blob/0d44a416b16b2781dcdc867f960871dabea9c1bf/scripts/test-manifest-sync.sh#L125-L140)

## Conclusion

Testing and deployment operations in GestaltView constitute a rigorous validation framework focused on preserving system invariants and ensuring high availability of AI services. By utilizing a multi-phased approach—from unit tests and TypeScript validation to specialized connectivity and manifest synchronization checks—the project maintains a verified "Resonance Loop" that is ready for secure and consistent deployment.
